# Frontend System Sweep — Durable Ledger

**Goal (started 2026-05-28, "goal mode" — spans many sessions):** Find and fix EVERY frontend bug, missing/incomplete frontend feature, and missing/incorrect frontend↔backend wiring across the whole GrantPipe system. Includes UI integration testing, UI system-integration testing, and full local E2E. Sub-agent driven. Multiple review/fix cycles until nothing remains.

**This file is the source of truth across sessions. READ IT FIRST. UPDATE IT CONTINUOUSLY.**

## Working context

- Worktree: `.worktrees/frontend-system-sweep` — branch `goal/frontend-system-sweep`
- Prior findings (status was unknown at sweep start; must re-validate): `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-25-frontend-audit-sweep.md` (50 items)
- Canon: **all buttons are pills** (`rounded-full`) — enforced at `packages/ui` Button/IconButton base.
- Required per CLAUDE.md: TDD, 95% coverage per touched file, no `any`, no TODO, review→fix→merge→remove worktree→deploy via Wrangler.

## How a session should operate

1. Read this ledger. Pick the next OPEN item(s) (or run a discovery wave if the inventory is stale/incomplete for an area).
2. Dispatch implementer subagent (TDD) for a non-overlapping file scope.
3. Spec-compliance review → fix. Code-quality review → fix.
4. Run the relevant tests + typecheck. Mark item DONE with commit SHA.
5. Periodically run UI integration + local E2E (Playwright/preview) to catch cross-feature regressions.
6. When an area is clean across two consecutive review cycles, mark the AREA verified.

## Status legend

`OPEN` not started · `WIP` in progress · `DONE` fixed+reviewed+tested · `VERIFIED` confirmed via UI/E2E · `WONTFIX` (with reason)

- WIRE-AUDITCSV-EXPORT P3 DONE(8e4fe5cd) — Surfaced as a `canManage`-gated pill "Export CSV" Button in the Reviewer-activity section header of `settings.portal-access.tsx`, disabled when no activity, with an error Alert, using `downloadViaOrgFetch("/api/external-reviewers/audit-events/export.csv", "reviewer-activity.csv")`. Wrote a full portal-access test suite (file now 99.77L/95.33F/100B) and DELETED its stale `verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs` baseline entry. Original finding: The external-reviewers backend exposes `GET /api/external-reviewers/audit-events/export.csv` (`requirePermission("compliance","view")`, tested in routes.test.ts, supports optional `?sessionId`) but NO frontend caller exists. `settings.portal-access.tsx` surfaces the audit trail as a JSON-backed "Reviewer activity" table (via `useAuditEvents`) with full loading/error/empty states, but offers no CSV download — so this is an un-surfaced backend convenience, NOT a dead affordance/broken promise (nothing in the UI claims an export). Legit small missing feature for the audit_ready compliance flow (hand an auditor a CSV of the access trail). FIX = pill "Export CSV" Button in the Reviewer-activity section header, `canManage`-gated, using `downloadViaOrgFetch("/api/external-reviewers/audit-events/export.csv", "reviewer-activity.csv")` (Wave 5 helper). **COST NOTE:** `settings.portal-access.tsx` is 1031 lines with a low coverage baseline (83.04L/62F/61.53B in verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs:84) — touching it trips the pre-commit flat-95×4 touched-file gate, forcing the WHOLE file to ≥95×4 (≈33pts of function coverage to add) AND deletion of its baseline entry. Disproportionate to bundle into a discovery wave; do as a dedicated fix wave that budgets for a full portal-access test suite.
- WIRE-PORTAL-REPORT-DOWNLOAD P2 DONE(d97f8e5a) — The reviewer portal generated-report page (`routes/portal/generated-reports.$id.tsx`) rendered report metadata but offered NO download, even though the backend exposes `GET /api/public/portal/generated-reports/:id/download` (cookie-authed, portalReviewerMiddleware + checkScope). Added a `portalGeneratedReportDownloadUrl()` helper to `use-portal-session.ts` and a pill `<Button asChild><a href=… download>Download</a></Button>` in the page header. Cookie session rides the same-origin anchor automatically — no X-Org-Id header needed (portal auth is cookie-based, unlike authenticated app downloads).
- WIRE-PORTAL-PROGRAM + WIRE-PORTAL-RESTRICTION P2 DONE(a6b7529d) — Two EXTERNAL_REVIEW_SCOPE_TYPES (`program`, `restriction_term`) had working backend portal routes (`GET /api/public/portal/programs/:id`, `/restriction-terms/:id`) and were emitted as scopes by `/me`, but `getScopeRoute` in `portal/home.tsx` had no case for them — so they rendered as disabled "coming soon" cards (dead-ends for the auditor). Added `usePortalProgram` + `usePortalRestrictionTerm` hooks, created `routes/portal/programs.$id.tsx` + `routes/portal/restriction-terms.$id.tsx` detail pages (loading/error/null + full field display, mirroring funds page), and wired both into `getScopeRoute` + the `ScopeRoute` union. Regenerated `routeTree.gen.ts` via `@tanstack/router-generator` programmatically (the `tsr` CLI on PATH is the unrelated ts-remove-unused tool — do NOT use it; the repo generates routes via the `TanStackRouterVite` vite plugin). All touched files 100% coverage. Note: the OTHER unmapped scope types (subaward, subrecipient\*, reimbursement_request, activity_log) have NO backend portal route, so their disabled cards are CORRECT and must stay.

### Web shell / routing / auth

- WEB-SHELL-01 P1 DONE(14a6cb3c) — Auditor sees out-of-scope nav (Calendar/Activity/Notifications) — `apps/web/src/config/nav.ts:120,167,301` gated on readOnlyRoles incl auditor. Fixed: Calendar/Activity/Notifications now use standardRoles (auditor excluded). Tests in nav.test.ts.
- WEB-SHELL-02 P1 DONE(14a6cb3c) — `AUDITOR_PERMISSIONS` granted programs/payments "view" → contradicted CLAUDE.md matrix — `packages/shared/src/types/index.ts`. Fixed: programs/payments → "none" for Auditor; canonical-matrix assertion test added in index.test.ts. (Cross-cutting: also tightens API authz.)
- WEB-SHELL-03 P1 DONE(f4b78f74) — Paywall bypass: minimal-shell early return (onboarding/select-plan/confirm-plan) moved to run AFTER the paywall block in `_authenticated.tsx`, so blocked (trial*expired/subscription*\*) users hitting those routes get the paywall recovery screen instead of slipping past. Allowed (active/trialing) users unaffected; admin billing-recovery via /settings still reachable. Tests: paywall-before-minimal-shell cases in `_authenticated.test.tsx`.
- WEB-SHELL-04 P2 DONE(f4b78f74) — Portal home loading state now `role=status` + `aria-live=polite` with sr-only announcement + animate-pulse skeleton + visible label — `portal/home.tsx`. New `portal/home.test.tsx` (17 tests) covers loading/error/no-data/loaded + getScopeRoute branches.
- WEB-SHELL-05 P2 DONE(f4b78f74) — Non-admin member of an un-onboarded org now sees a blocking "workspace is still being set up" screen (admins still redirect to /onboarding). Gate fires only for KNOWN non-admin roles (`userRole != null && !== "admin"`), never for an unresolved role. Sign-out path covered. Tests in `_authenticated.test.tsx`.
- WEB-SHELL-06 P2 DONE(f4b78f74) — AppShell `<main id="main-content">` is now focusable (`tabIndex={-1}`) in both default + minimal variants so the skip-link target receives focus — `components/shell/app-shell.tsx`. Tests in `app-shell.test.tsx`.

### Cross-cutting — frontend↔backend wiring

> Discovery wave 2 (2026-05-29, agent abbd8c73): audited full round trip (hook→RPC→route→service→validator) for settings/team, billing/Stripe, import/CSV, documents/R2. All four largely correctly wired (payload shapes match validators; post-mutation invalidation thorough; role-gating consistent). One defect class found + a sibling instance located by orchestrator follow-up.

- WIRE-DOCS-01 P2 DONE (01a DONE / 01b DONE) — Authenticated file downloads used a bare `<a href>` that cannot send the `X-Org-Id` header, so multi-org users who switched active org got the API's fallback (most-recently-joined) org → wrong-org artifact / "not found". Two sites, both verified org-scoped on the API via `c.get("orgId")` (resolved from `X-Org-Id` header w/ MRU fallback in `apps/api/src/app.ts:300-323`):
  - (a) DONE(9c4c64b7) — `apps/web/src/components/entity-documents-section.tsx` → `GET /api/documents/:id/download`. Bare `<a>` swapped to a pill `<Button variant="link">` calling the new `downloadViaOrgFetch` helper with in-flight disable + inline destructive error box. New `apps/web/src/lib/download.ts` (14 tests, 100×4) + `entity-documents-section.tsx` (100/97.87/100/100). Pre-commit gate green.
  - (b) DONE(e4249d83) — `apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/reports/$reportId.tsx` `artifact.downloadPath` download (`GET /api/compliance/reports/:reportId/download`). Bare anchor → pill `<Button variant="outline">` calling `downloadViaOrgFetch` via `handleDownload(readyArtifact)` (param-passed to drop the dead `!artifact` guard) with `isDownloading` disable + inline destructive `downloadError` box. `$reportId.tsx` lifted to 100×4; removed its `{ functions: 83.33 }` baseline entry from `verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs`; also updated the cross-page `reports-pages.test.tsx` (3 stale `link`→`button` assertions). Pre-commit gate green.
  - Helper: shared `apps/web/src/lib/download.ts` `fetch`es with `createOrgRequestInit` (`org-context.ts:34`), reads the blob, derives filename from `Content-Disposition` (fallback provided), triggers an object-URL download, surfaces errors. Portal downloads (`/api/public/portal/...`, token-scoped) are NOT affected — left alone.

> Discovery wave 3 (2026-05-29, agent a55f5b7d): audited dashboard, notifications, calendar, activity log, custom fields, donors/grants/funds mutation round trips. All correctly wired EXCEPT the two notification findings below. Confirmed NON-defects: ContactForm edit reuses `createContactSchema` but `updateContactSchema` accepts every emitted field (no data loss); no new bare-anchor downloads beyond WIRE-DOCS-01's two sites.

- WIRE-NOTIF-01 P2 DONE (Wave 6, 4d400c17) — Topbar bell never surfaces the unread count. `apps/web/src/components/shell/app-topbar.tsx:67-71` renders a static `<Link to="/notifications">` Bell with no badge; the working `useUnreadNotificationCount` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-notifications.ts:101-109` → `GET /api/notifications/unread-count`) is consumed ONLY on the notifications page (`notifications.tsx:82`), so unread items are invisible app-wide. Fix: call `useUnreadNotificationCount()` in app-topbar and overlay a count badge on the Bell IconButton when `unreadCount > 0`.
- WIRE-NOTIF-02 P3 DONE (Wave 6, 4d400c17) — Notifications never poll. `use-notifications.ts:75-88` (`useNotifications`) and `:101-109` (`useUnreadNotificationCount`) set no `refetchInterval` (cf. `use-document-extractions.ts:77` which polls), so server-raised notifications (e.g. deadline crossings) never appear without a navigation/manual refetch. Fix: add `refetchInterval: 60_000` to `useUnreadNotificationCount` (and optionally `useNotifications`). Compounds with WIRE-NOTIF-01 (a badge without polling is itself stale). Pair these two into one implementer wave.

> Discovery wave 4 (2026-05-29, agent a1bfece3): audited the compliance/report-generation flow end-to-end (web reports pages/hooks ↔ api compliance + restrictions domains ↔ shared validators). Payload shapes for all six generation hooks match their Zod validators exactly; errors surfaced via Alert; query invalidation on every mutation is correct; RPC calls carry `X-Org-Id` via the typed client. Independently RE-CONFIRMED WIRE-DOCS-01b (the reports/$reportId.tsx bare-anchor download — already in flight). Two NEW defects + one P3:

- WIRE-REPORT-POLL P2 DONE (Wave 7, 9254e574) — Report detail never polls for status transitions. `useReportArtifact` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-reports.ts:88-97`) has no `refetchInterval`. After generating a report, `handleGeneratedReportMutation` navigates straight to `/reports/$reportId`, where the artifact is often still `queued`/`generating`; the page shows the static "still generating" message and the download button never appears until a manual refresh. Same class as WIRE-NOTIF-02. Fix: conditional `refetchInterval` on `useReportArtifact` — poll (~3000ms) while `status` is `queued`/`generating`, else `false`. Touched-file gate: lift `use-reports.ts` to ≥95×4 if below. Pair naturally with WIRE-DOCS-01b (same area) or its own small wave.
- WIRE-ROLLFWD P2 DONE (Wave 7, 9254e574) — Fragile untyped rollforward id extraction. `apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/reports/index.tsx:562-564` casts the `useGenerateRestrictedRollforward` result (`{ report, rows, evidencePackage }`, restrictions service ~`:1198`) as `{ report?: { id?: string } }` and falls back to `""`; an undefined `report.id` silently navigates to `/reports/` (empty param) with no error shown. Fix: import the real return type from the restrictions service/shared package, use a typed accessor, and throw/surface an error if the id is empty instead of navigating to a broken route. Touched-file gate applies to index.tsx.
- WIRE-DOCS-02 P2 DONE (Wave 9, 0d69bdd3) — Subrecipient monitoring Documents panel was a dead-end list. `apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/subrecipients/$subrecipientId.tsx` rendered `detail.documents` as `{filename, entityType}` cards with NO download action, even though each item carries `document.id` and the generic org-scoped endpoint `/api/documents/:id/download` works for any attached doc (confirmed Wave 5: both routes read `c.get("orgId")`). Fixed: per-doc download Button using `downloadViaOrgFetch(`/api/documents/${id}/download`, filename)` with in-flight `downloadingDocumentId` disable + inline destructive error box — mirrors the Wave 5 entity-documents-section pattern. NEW `$subrecipientId.test.tsx`(35 tests, coverage 100L/100S/100F/95.94B) written for the previously-untested ~600-line page; its`{0,0,0,0}`baseline entry deleted from verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs. Necessary dead-code cleanup for branch coverage: removed the unreachable post-early-return "Upgrade required" Alert and simplified an always-true`canUseMonitoring &&` guard.
- WIRE-CUSTOMFIELD-DONATION P2 DONE (Wave 10, 880a2b3d) — Custom fields defined for donations had no data-entry surface. The settings UI (`custom-fields-settings-section.tsx`) advertises "Define additional fields that appear on contacts, donations, and grants" and exposes a **Donations** definition tab; the generic org route `/custom-fields/:entityType/:entityId/values` validates against all three `CUSTOM_FIELD_ENTITY_TYPES` (contact, donation, grant). But only `grants/$grantId.tsx` (grant) and `donors/$contactId.tsx` (contact) rendered `EntityCustomFieldsSection` — a donation custom field could be created but never filled in (broken promise). Fixed: render `<EntityCustomFieldsSection entityType="donation" entityId={editingDonation.id} />` inside the Edit Donation dialog on `$contactId.tsx` (existing donations have an id; new-donation creation has none). NEW test scopes the assertion to the open dialog via `within(getByTestId("donation-form").closest('[role="dialog"]'))` because the inactive contact custom-fields tab panel stays mounted (Radix) and also renders an empty section. `$contactId.tsx` already exceeded flat 95×4 (98.67L/95.9B/95.65F/98.67S) so its stale `{functions:86.36,branches:89.28}` baseline entry was deleted from verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs. NOTE for future: `donation` + `award_intake` also lack a DOCUMENT_ENTITY_TYPES document surface, but documents-on-donations is not advertised in any UI, so it is an enhancement, not a broken-promise defect — not queued.
- WIRE-REPORT-PERM P3 NOTE(likely WONTFIX) — The six generate-report buttons on `reports/index.tsx` are gated on plan tier + input state but not on `reports.view`/`compliance.view` client-side. API enforces via `requireAllPermissions`, and per the CLAUDE.md roles matrix Viewers/Auditors MAY generate reports, so ungated buttons are mostly policy-correct; only the rare user lacking BOTH perms sees enabled buttons that 403. Low value; revisit only if it surfaces in E2E.

There is no `/downloads/` Astro route; actual PDF delivery is via signed time-limited R2 URLs (DOWNLOAD_LINK_SECRET) emailed by the leads domain. The R2 storage key `lead-magnets/{slug}.pdf` is a separate concern from this route identifier.

## Session log

- 2026-06-14 — WAVE 216 (VERIFICATION + RECON wave — no code change; pivot to the directive's core unfinished phase, live e2e visual verification). OUTCOME: entry-screen visual audit PASSED. Drove a real browser (Playwright) against a local web dev server and captured full-page screenshots of `/login`, `/forgot-password`, `/signup` at 1440×900 plus `/signup` at 390×844 (mobile). All clean, consistent, on-brand: left emerald value-prop panel + right white card, pill CTAs (Sign in / Send reset link / Start trial / Continue with Google), correct cross-links, mobile collapses the panel and goes full-width with no overflow/clipping; 0 console errors on every page (the 61 console "warnings" are all benign `[tanstack-router] …will not be code-split` dev hints — see below — NOT errors). No shippable entry-screen defect found. BLOCKERS that prevented AUTHENTICATED e2e this wave (both real, both outside my safe control): (1) the canonical local stack ports 3050 (web) + 5050 (api) are held by a concurrent agent session; local Better Auth sign-in only works from origin localhost:3050, and hitting that session's stack would mutate its shared DB/login state (Process Safety) — my own servers fell back to web:3053 / api:8788, which can't satisfy the auth origin; (2) production e2e via the GRANTPIPE*E2E*_ account is blocked because typing the password through an MCP fill tool would expose it in the transcript, violating the standing "never paste the password" constraint. RECON FINDING (deferred, documented to PREVENT future churn): all 61 route files under `apps/web/src/routes/**` defeat TanStack Router `autoCodeSplitting: true` (confirmed enabled in apps/web/vite.config.ts:39) by adding a NAMED export of their page component (e.g. `export function DashboardPage()`), which the plugin warns "will not be code-split and will increase your bundle size". Removing the export would code-split them out of the critical bundle, BUT every route test imports that named component (e.g. `import { JournalIndexPage } from "./index"`), and `routeFileIgnorePattern` only ignores `_.test/\*.spec`— so a sibling page module CANNOT live in`src/routes/**`(it would itself become a route). The proper fix = relocate each page component OUT of`src/routes/**`(e.g.`src/features/<x>/<x>-page.tsx`) and repoint route+test imports: a heavy, risky, multi-wave structural refactor. NOT URGENT — recent entry chunk 861.1 kB is under the 862,000 guard — so deferred rather than churned across 61 files speculatively in a contended environment. CLEANUP: killed only my own stray dev servers (vite PID 42800 on :3053, workerd PID 46728 on :8788 — verified via Win32_Process CommandLine before Stop-Process; never touched the canonical 3050/5050); created then removed the unused `.worktrees/sweep-w216` worktree + branch (no code changes were made in it). NEXT: the authenticated-e2e phase needs either an exclusive local stack (ports 3050/5050 free) or a password-safe auth path; until then continue shipping concrete improvements. If pursuing the code-split refactor later, do it as scoped per-feature waves with a build-output entry-chunk delta to prove each batch's win.

DEFECT (verified firsthand by reading source + tests + RED): `formatDate` in `apps/web/src/components/entity-documents-section.tsx` (lines 47-53) formatted a document's ISO-UTC `createdAt` via `toLocaleDateString("en-US", {year,month,day})` with NO `timeZone: "UTC"` — so a doc uploaded between 00:00 UTC and the local offset renders as the PRIOR calendar day for any user in a negative-UTC-offset zone (all US TZs). Confirmed RED (rendered "Mar 31, 2026" → getByText fail), then GREEN; full file 23/23. NOTE on the TZ-forcing pattern: Node re-reads `process.env.TZ` per Intl/Date call, so setting it at runtime then calling toLocaleDateString makes the test deterministic regardless of the CI runner's default TZ — this is the reliable RED-then-GREEN pattern for timezone bugs in this repo (no global TZ is set in vitest config; other date tests rely on source-level `timeZone:"UTC"`). - 2026-06-14 — WAVE 199 (stale contact giving-history fund name after a fund soft-delete) COMPLETE. Cherry-picked commit 116c3744 (originally 4b1aa5e7); deploy:web Version 06584b9a-e280-4f9a-926c-a5f4ae30030d (DEPLOY_EXIT=0). Flavor-A stale-cache defect on DELETE, candidate (b) reserved at the end of Wave 198 and verified firsthand. `deleteFund.onSuccess` (`use-grants.ts:662-675`, `useFundUpdateMutations`) invalidated `["fund",id]`(remove)/`["funds"]`/`["grant"]`/`["grants"]`/overview but NOT `["donations"]`. `listDonations` (`donors/donation.service.ts:244-257`) selects `fundName: funds.name` via a leftJoin whose ON condition INCLUDES `isNull(funds.deletedAt)` — so soft-deleting a fund makes the join fail and `fundName` resolves to NULL, flipping the value the contact giving-history table renders (`donors/$contactId.tsx:995` `{donation.fundName ?? "N/A"}`) from the fund name to "N/A". The query key is `["donations", contactId, page, pageSize]` (`use-donors.ts:203`); the `["donations"]` prefix covers it. The sibling `updateFund.onSuccess` already invalidates `["donations"]` (`use-grants.ts:646-654`, with the same comment) for the rename case; deleteFund diverged. FIX: added `void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["donations"] })` (with comment) to deleteFund.onSuccess after the `["grants"]` line, before invalidateOverview. TDD: added a `["donations"]` assertion (with comment) to the ISOLATED deleteFund test (`use-grants.test.ts` ~1024, which clears mockInvalidateQueries at ~1011 — so the assertion is meaningful, not masked by the earlier updateFund call in the same test that also invalidates `["donations"]`); verified RED (1 failed — `["donations"]` absent) then GREEN (44/44 in file). build-output.test passed with NO guard bump. Prior vite build (BUILD_EXIT=0); full web typecheck+coverage gate green IN THE WORKTREE ("Web source coverage is >= 95% per file, except documented legacy baselines."; 6/6 tasks, GATE_EXIT=0). Self-review of /tmp/w199.diff PASS (key exactly `["donations"]`, correct placement inside onSuccess, matching isolated assertion, `void` present, no `any`/TODO/console.log — a one-line mirror of the already-shipped updateFund sibling; lite/haiku reviewer couldn't resolve the /tmp path in its sandbox, so reviewed the full inline diff directly). **MERGE WRINKLE (resolved):** during the completion sequence master had advanced two API commits (645377d6/fb896d71, Sentry hardening, from a concurrent session/another computer), so `git merge --ff-only sweep-w199` aborted ("Not possible to fast-forward"); the chained `git branch -D sweep-w199` still ran and deleted the branch, leaving 4b1aa5e7 dangling but intact in the object store. Recovered by cherry-picking 4b1aa5e7 onto master (clean — web-only change, no overlap with the API commits) → 116c3744. The web gate result still held (the two new API commits don't touch apps/web). LESSON: when ff-merge can fail (multi-computer repo), don't `;`-chain `git branch -D` after it — gate the branch delete on merge success, or cherry-pick. deploy:web via Wrangler (NOTE: the two API Sentry commits on master were NOT deployed by this session — they predate my checkout and belong to whoever authored them; I deployed only my affected app, web). The fund→donation denormalized-name surface is now FULLY covered (updateFund rename W-prior + deleteFund delete W199) — do NOT re-hunt it. **NON-DEFECT confirmed this Wave (do NOT fix): `deleteGrant`→`["payment-requests"]`** — candidate (a) from the W198 hunt was DISPROVEN firsthand: `deleteGrant` (`grant.service.ts:739-768`) only soft-deletes the grant row (no cascade to payment requests), and `listPaymentRequests` (`payments/request.service.ts:28-72`) filters only `isNull(grantPaymentRequests.deletedAt)` while its grants leftJoin does NOT filter `grants.deletedAt` — so after a grant delete, its payment requests REMAIN in the list and `grantName` STILL resolves to the (soft-deleted) grant's name → the payments list output is unchanged → no staleness. (The Explore agent missed that the join ignores deletedAt; trust-but-verify caught it.) Next: WAVE 200 — the cache-staleness vein is heavily mined (the W200 pre-hunt re-found ONLY this W199 defect and nothing new). Run a deeper/wider hunt on truly unexplored surfaces (pledges/recurring gifts, subawards, compliance/reporting-deadline embeds, document labels on entity detail, notification badges, custom-field-definition rename→values display, tags/segments membership, restrictions, trial-balance/other accounting reports, contact→pledge/soft-credit embeds, expense-category rename) AND/OR PIVOT to a fresh non-cache defect class (form validation, empty/loading/error states, a11y/focus management/keyboard nav, responsive layout, pill-button canon compliance, number/currency formatting precision). Apply the strict DELETE false-positive guard: a delete is only a defect if the read's join filters the entity's deletedAt OR the read drops the soft-deleted entity's rows (output genuinely changes).

- 2026-06-14 — WAVE 189 (stale event-detail revenue total after editing or deleting a donation from the donor page) COMPLETE. Commit ff3cf182; deploy:web via Wrangler. Flavor-B stale-cache defect (correlated-aggregate embed across entities). The event detail page renders the event's total revenue via `formatCurrency(event.summary?.revenueCents)` (`apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/events/$eventId.tsx:530`) from the `["event", eventId]` query (`use-events.ts:82` `useEvent`). The backend `getEvent` embeds `with: { attendees: { with: { contact: true, donation: true } }, volunteerHours: true }` and computes `summary.revenueCents` in `withSummary` (`apps/api/src/domains/events/event.service.ts:50-80`) by summing each ACTIVE attendee's `attendee.donation.amountCents` (skips `donation.deletedAt != null`, dedups by donation.id). Flavor-B stale-cache defect (helper-vs-twin divergence — the same systematic lead w187 surfaced). The donations import commit posts a journal entry for EVERY imported donation server-side: `apps/api/src/domains/import/service.ts:887-892` calls `postDonation(db, { orgId, actorId, donationId, action: "create" })` per inserted row. So a donations import changes exactly the data all six Accounting views read: the journal-entries list (`accounting/journal/index.tsx:46` `useJournalEntries`, key `["accounting-journal-entries", ...]` at `use-accounting.ts:402-412`), trial balance (`accounting/trial-balance.tsx:63`, key `["accounting-trial-balance", ...]` at `use-accounting.ts:485`), ledger (`["accounting-ledger"]`), and the three financial reports (`["accounting-report-financial-position"]`, `["accounting-report-activities"]`, `["accounting-report-functional-expenses"]`). But `commitImport.onSuccess` (`use-imports.ts:231-244`, driven by `importEntityQueryKeys.donations` at lines 93-101) invalidated only `["donations"]`, `["contacts"]`, `["dashboard-overview"]`, `["donor-stats"]`, `["retention-stats"]`, `["activity"]`, `["org-activity"]` — NONE prefix-match any accounting key. So after a donations CSV import, every Accounting page kept showing the pre-import state (journal entries missing, trial balance / reports omitting the posted amounts) until a hard reload. The standalone twin `useCreateDonation.onSuccess` (`use-donors.ts:236`) already refreshes all six via the shared `invalidateDonationAccountingViews` (`use-donors.ts:51-54` → `["accounting-journal-entries"]` + `invalidateAccountingBalanceViews` at `use-accounting.ts:35-41` for the other five) — the import path makes the identical `postDonation` backend change but had diverged, never refreshing accounting. Contacts/grants/grant_opportunities key sets left unchanged (those imports don't post journal entries). Lite/haiku review (reading /tmp/w188.diff, with codebase cross-check) PASS (all six key strings match the codebase exactly, each appears once in test + once in impl in identical order, no duplicate keys / syntax errors, comments accurate). ff-merged to master, branch deleted, worktree unregistered (`git worktree prune`; leftover dir busy from a lingering handle — `rm -rf .worktrees/sweep-w188` retried at deploy). deploy:web via Wrangler. **NON-DEFECTS cleared this hunt (Explore-surfaced, firsthand-verified):** `useLinkAttendeeDonation` not calling `invalidateDonationAccountingViews` — correct, `linkAttendeeDonation` only sets `donationId` on the attendee row, it does NOT call `postDonation` (`attendee.service.ts:276-309`); contacts import missing `["retention-stats"]` — adding contacts without donations doesn't affect repeat-donor retention; grant import vs `invalidateGrant`'s specific `["grant", id]` key — N/A since import creates new grants whose IDs are unknown at invalidation time, `["grants"]` prefix covers them. Next: WAVE 189 — continue stale-cache defect hunt; the helper-vs-twin strategy is now well-mined (attendee-donation→contacts w187, donations-import→accounting w188). Candidate surfaces: expense/allocation import or bulk paths vs their standalone mutation's accounting/fund-balance invalidations, donation DELETE/UPDATE import-or-bulk paths vs accounting reversal invalidations, pledge/recurring-gift mutations → derived totals, OR pivot to a fresh non-cache defect class (form validation, empty/loading/error states, a11y, responsive layout, focus management, keyboard nav).

No backend change needed — the server already enforced the permission; this closes the frontend affordance gap so viewers aren't shown actions they can't perform.

- 2026-05-29 — WAVE 25 (WEB-FUND-TYPE-COLDLOAD silent fund-type corruption) COMPLETE. Commit 70b85109 (3 files). **DEFECT (high severity, silent data corruption):** `funds/$fundId.tsx` initialized the fund-type Select via a lazy `useState(() => fundQuery.data?.type)` that read the query ONCE at first render. On a cold load (query still pending) it locked to the `"unrestricted"` default; when the real fund resolved, the Select stayed stale, and the save handler's diff `...(fundTypeDraft !== currentType ? { type: fundTypeDraft } : {})` then silently PATCHed `type:"unrestricted"` over the real type whenever the user saved ANY other edit (e.g. a name change) without touching Type. FIX (TDD): replaced the lazy-init state with **derived state** — track only an explicit user override (`fundTypeOverride`), compute `fundTypeDraft = fundTypeOverride ?? loadedFundType ?? "unrestricted"`, Select `onValueChange` sets the override. **TWO LESSONS:** (1) the FIRST fix attempt used a `useEffect` to sync the state and was BLOCKED by the React 19 lint rule `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` ("can trigger cascading renders") — the commit did NOT land; the derived-state refactor (no effect) is both lint-clean and the React-recommended "you might not need an effect" pattern. The CANONICAL alternative when an effect truly is needed is the grants/$grantId.tsx dirty-flag functional-updater sync (lines 763-785), confirmed already-correct and NOT buggy. (2) jsdom won't submit a form with an empty `required` field, so the nullish-fallback arms behind required inputs (224-225, 231) are unreachable via the submit path — final coverage 100/95.38/100/100 with those dead arms documented. Added 4 tests (29 total): cold-load sync, name-only change, type-change via real Radix Select (`getByRole("combobox",{name:"Type"})`→`findByRole("option",{name:"Temporarily Restricted"})`), typeless fallback. Deleted the file's `{functions:71.42,branches:91.8}` baseline from verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs. Confirmed no other instance of the lazy-useState-from-query antipattern in the donors/grants/funds mutation surface. Pre-commit full web coverage gate green. Next: DISCOVERY — grants/$grantId.tsx deep mutation round-trips (allocations, expenses, impact metrics, closeout items), then paywall/portal deeper, then full local E2E.
- 2026-05-29 — WAVE 26 (WEB-GRANT-EMPTYOPT blank optional fields rejected by server) COMPLETE. Commit 7137d529 (3 files). **DEFECT (high severity, silent failure of the common case):** in `grants/$grantId.tsx`, three create handlers always included an optional text key whose value was `""` when the user left it blank — expense `description` (~1597), impact-metric `unit` (~1736), metric-entry `notes` (~1819). The shared `optionalTrimmedString = z.string().trim().min(1).optional()` (packages/shared/src/validators/grants.ts:14) is optional-but-NOT-nullable and `min(1)`, so the server's `zValidator("json", …)` (routes.ts createGrantExpenseSchema/createImpactMetricSchema/createImpactMetricEntrySchema) rejects `""` with a 400. Net effect: adding an expense with no description, a metric with no unit, or an entry with no note silently 400'd — and the metric/entry handlers had NO try/catch, so it was an unhandled rejection (no user-visible error). FIX: spread-conditional omit each field when empty (`...(x.length > 0 ? { x } : {})`); the matching CREATE validators accept the omitted/undefined field. **LESSON (validator contract, reusable):** the `optionalTrimmedString` pattern means a blank optional text field must be OMITTED, never sent as `""` and never sent as `null` (it is not `.nullable()`). UPDATE handlers that use `nullableOptionalString` are the exception — they accept `null` to clear. The expense handler already had try/catch→expenseError; left the metric/unit and entry/notes handlers' error surfacing as-is (primary fix is the payload; their inputs are inline and the omit removes the only 400 trigger for blank-optional). Added 6 tests (134 total) covering both arms (omit-when-blank + include-when-present) for all three fields; used `expect.not.objectContaining({ field: expect.anything() })` and exact `toHaveBeenCalledWith` rather than `.mock.calls[0][0]` (TS strict flags `calls[0]` possibly-undefined). Full-suite coverage for the file rose to 99.07/95.68/98.18/99.07 — deleted its `{lines:93.66,functions:88,branches:94.97,statements:93.66}` baseline from verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs (now flat 95×4). Pre-commit full web coverage gate green. Next: continue grants/$grantId.tsx remaining surfaces (PaymentsGrantTab, spend-down/generate-report) then paywall/portal deeper, then full local E2E.

### Database / migrations (NEW area — surfaced by live local E2E) — Wave 54

- DB-MIGRATE-01 P1 FIXED — **Fresh `drizzle-kit migrate` was BROKEN; aborted at migration 0017** with `relation "lead_magnet_downloads" already exists`. Root cause: a merge/renumber collision. `0014_panoramic_prowler.sql` (from master) creates `lead_magnet_downloads`, `lead_nurture_schedule`, `leads` + both FKs + 3 indexes; `0015_omniscient_darkhawk.sql` then converts `lead_nurture_schedule.step`→smallint and `leads.email`→citext. `0017_audit_remediation_indexes.sql` (from the audit-remediation branch) then re-CREATEs the SAME 3 tables (citext/smallint already) + the SAME 2 FKs + SAME 3 indexes — pure duplicates of the post-0015 state — alongside its genuinely-unique work (DROP notifications dedupe index, bigint amount widenings, org_id/journal_lines/fiscal_periods indexes, notifications dedupe recreate). On a FRESH DB the duplicate CREATE TABLE aborts migrate at 17/57, so signup→org-bootstrap hit `column "permissions" does not exist` (added by 0029, never reached). Prod unaffected: drizzle-orm's migrator applies by `when`-timestamp watermark, not by re-hashing applied files, so editing an already-applied migration's SQL does not re-run it on prod. The lead tables are transient anyway — altered at 0023/0024 and dropped entirely at 0028 (`remove_neon_marketing_tables`); schema source has no leads tables. FIX: removed 0017's duplicate lead-table section (the 3 CREATE TABLEs + 2 FKs + 3 lead indexes), keeping all its unique statements. Fresh chain now: 0014 creates tables → 0015 retypes → 0017 (indexes/bigint only) → 0023 drops lead_step_unique (exists from 0014) → 0024 → 0028 drops. Snapshots untouched so `drizzle generate` stays clean. Regression guard: apps/api migration-chain test that applies all migrations to a throwaway schema and asserts completion + org_members.permissions present. UNBLOCKS all local E2E for this goal.

- DB-MIGRATE-01 — COMPLETE & MERGED (commit ff50bcb4 on master, fast-forward). Two collisions, not one: (1) 0014↔0017 duplicate lead-table CREATEs (above); (2) **0017↔0022** — `0022_rapid_black_tom.sql` also re-issued 0017's DROP+recreate of `notifications_org_user_dedupe_unique`, the donations/events bigint widenings, and `CREATE INDEX "contacts_org_id_idx"`. Because drizzle-kit runs DDL **without a per-migration transaction**, 0022's first statement (DROP notifications index) committed, then its duplicate `CREATE contacts_org_id_idx` aborted the run, leaving the dedupe index dropped — a second, separate break after 0017 was fixed. FIX: reduced 0022 to its single unique statement (`ALTER TABLE "activity_log" ADD COLUMN "entity_label"`). Regression guards live in **packages/db/src/migrations.test.ts** (static, docker-free, 2 new tests → 18 total): (a) no table is plain-`CREATE TABLE`d more than once across the chain (IF NOT EXISTS exempt); (b) index-DDL replay across the chain rejects a plain CREATE on an already-existing index or a plain DROP on a missing one. TDD-verified: guard (b) fails on the original 0022 with `CREATE INDEX "contacts_org_id_idx" but it already exists`, passes on the fix. Live end-to-end verification on freshly-migrated local docker DB (drop public+drizzle schemas → migrate): exit=0, applied=57, 102 tables, org_members.permissions=jsonb; `generate` reports no drift. Real signup POST /api/auth/better/sign-up/email → 200 + session cookies; user.create.after `createOrgForUser` bootstrapped org + org_members(role=admin, permissions jsonb). Reviewed (APPROVE, no changes), merged to master, worktree retained as the ongoing goal workspace. No Wrangler app deploy required (db-only migration-chain edit; prod already has these applied via the when-timestamp watermark). It is NOT a data/record count. The Accounting section (`config/nav.ts`, `defaultCollapsed: true`) genuinely contains 12 nav links (Overview, Chart of Accounts, Journal, Ledger, Trial Balance, …), so "12" is correct and org-data-independent. ### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 56

- ONB-OVERLAY-OVERLAP P2 FIXED & MERGED (commit ca24e7c9 on master, fast-forward; deployed grantpipe-web). Live local E2E of the fresh-org dashboard (web 3050) surfaced a layout collision: TWO onboarding surfaces render simultaneously — the inline `OnboardingChecklist` card ("Start with these steps", guide-progress-backed, 5 items) and the floating `OnboardingOverlay` ("Your first 30 days", fixed `bottom-6 right-6 z-50`, localStorage `gp-ob-v1`-backed, 4-week guide). On a new org both appear, and the fixed overlay card sits on top of / covers the inline checklist's "Start / Mark done / Dismiss" action buttons in the lower-right, making them hard to click. The inline checklist is the primary, actionable onboarding surface, so the floating guide should yield to it. FIX: extracted a shared exported helper `getOpenChecklistItems(progress, role)` in `onboarding-checklist.tsx` (filters CHECKLIST_ITEMS by role then by not-completed/not-dismissed status) and refactored the component's own `openItems` to use it; in `onboarding-overlay.tsx` added `useGuideProgress()` + a `checklistActive` gate (`getOpenChecklistItems(...).length > 0`) folded into the existing early return `if (!visible || shouldSuppressByRole || checklistActive) return null;`. The 30-day overlay now stays hidden while any checklist item is still open and returns once the checklist is cleared/dismissed. All hooks remain called unconditionally before the early return (React rules preserved). TDD: `onboarding-overlay.test.tsx` +2 tests (suppressed when `mockGuideProgress=[]` → all items open; renders again when all 5 keys completed) with a new `useGuideProgress` mock + `mockGuideProgress` default of all-keys-completed in `beforeEach` so existing visible-overlay tests stay green (28 overlay tests pass, 25 checklist tests pass). SIBLING-MOCK GATE FIX: `dashboard.test.tsx` renders the real overlay (only the checklist component is stubbed), so the overlay's new `useGuideProgress` call hit "No QueryClient set" — added `vi.mock("../../hooks/use-guide-progress")` returning `{data: []}` and a `getOpenChecklistItems: () => []` export to the existing onboarding-checklist mock (54 dashboard tests pass). Per-file coverage: onboarding-overlay.tsx + onboarding-checklist.tsx combined 100/96.15/100/100 (flat-95×4). GATE: pre-commit full web `test:coverage` green (~10m35s incl. build-output.test.ts — built web first per the Wave-54 gotcha); reverted no generated-file churn this time. Reviewed (APPROVE, no must-fix; confirmed not a tautology and hook rules intact), merged to master, worktree removed, deployed to production grantpipe-web.

### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 57

- SUBR-EMPTY-STATE P2 FIXED & MERGED (commit 34f9a229 on master, fast-forward; deployed grantpipe-web version d7b77a47). Live sweep of `/app/subrecipients` (web 3050) on a fresh org surfaced a first-run UX defect: the subrecipients list ALWAYS rendered the filtered-empty `TeachAndActEmptyState` ("No subrecipients match this view." / "Try clearing your filters." / "Clear filters" button) whenever `rows.length === 0`, even for a brand-new org with NO data and NO active filters. Result: a dead "Clear filters" button (nothing to clear) and no path to create the first record — inconsistent with the Donors/Funders/Events "Your X live here" + create-CTA first-run pattern. FIX (`subrecipients/index.tsx`): added a derived `hasActiveFilters = search.trim() !== "" || status !== "all" || riskRating !== "all" || overdueTasks || openFindings` and split the empty branch into two: (1) filtered-empty (`rows===0 && !isLoading && hasActiveFilters`) keeps the existing "No subrecipients match this view." + Clear filters; (2) first-run (`rows===0 && !isLoading`, no filters) renders a new `TeachAndActEmptyState` heading "Your subrecipients live here", description "Track the groups you fund. Watch their risk. Keep audit findings in one place.", `primaryAction` = `canCreate ? {label:"Add your first subrecipient", onClick:()=>setCreateOpen(true)} : {label:"Open help", href:"/help"}` (read-only users get the help link, not a dead button), and `helpLink` "How subrecipients work". Branch order is filtered-empty → first-run → loading → grid so no dead-button state can render. CTA label is DELIBERATELY distinct from the PageHeader "Add subrecipient" trigger to avoid an ambiguous `getByRole("button", {name:"Add subrecipient"})` collision in the existing create-form tests (both wire to the same `createOpen`-controlled Dialog). TDD: `subrecipients/index.test.tsx` — rewrote the old "renders TeachAndActEmptyState when rows is empty" test to assert the first-run heading + CTA, +3 new tests (first-run CTA opens the create dialog via `within(region)`; filtered-empty shown — not first-run — when a search filter is active; read-only first-run shows "Open help" link not the Add button via `mockCanAccessFeature.mockReturnValue(false)`). 42/42 pass; per-file coverage index.tsx 100/96.49/100/100 (flat-95×4; uncovered branch 148-152 pre-existing). COPY: new empty-state strings passed the required `humanizer` (no AI tells; mirrors Funders canon) + `third-grade-copy` (FK grade 2.8, avg 5.2 words, max 9; split the original 18-word three-clause description into three short sentences). GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha), restored a spurious 902/902 CRLF-only `routeTree.gen.ts` churn before staging, pre-commit full web `test:coverage` green. Reviewed (APPROVE, no blockers), merged to master, worktree removed (rm -rf + prune, node_modules lock), deployed to production grantpipe-web. SWEEP NOTE: subrecipients surface now matches the canonical first-run empty-state pattern; 0 console errors on render confirmed pre-fix.

### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 58

- PAY-EMPTY-STATE P2 FIXED & MERGED (commit 840b333e on master, fast-forward). Live sweep of `/app/payments` (Cash, web 3050) on a fresh org surfaced the same first-run UX defect class as Wave 57: the payments first-run empty state set `primaryAction={{label:"Open help", href:"/help"}}`, which DUPLICATED the `helpLink` "How payment requests work" (also `/help`) — two links to the same place — and offered NO create path even though the header "New request" button was enabled for users who can create. Lifted it to a controlled/uncontrolled prop pattern (`open = controlledOpen ?? internalOpen`, `setOpen = onOpenChange ?? setInternalOpen`) and lifted `createOpen` to `PaymentsCashWorkspaceContent`, initialized from `Boolean(searchGrantId)` to preserve the auto-open-with-prefilled-grant behavior. The header dialog now binds `open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}`, and the empty-state `primaryAction` became `canCreateRequests ? {label:"Create your first request", onClick:()=>setCreateOpen(true)} : {label:"Open help", href:"/help"}` (read-only users still get the help link). `helpLink` unchanged. COPY: new CTA "Create your first request" passed `third-grade-copy` (FK 0.7, verb-led, 4 words; mirrors the Subrecipients/Funders create-CTA canon). Live sweep of `/app/activity` (Activity Log, web 3050) on the sweep org (1 entry present) surfaced a casing defect: each entry renders inline as an actor-verb-target sentence (`<span>{actorName}</span> <span>{action}</span> <span>{entityLabel}</span>`), but the action span applied `.replace(/_/g," ").replace(/^\w/, c=>c.toUpperCase())`, so the verb was force-capitalized MID-SENTENCE — "Sweep Tester Created Jordan" instead of the natural "Sweep Tester created Jordan". Reads like a typo and undercuts the "trust through precision" polish bar. The sibling `entity-activity-section.tsx` (line 186) intentionally `titleCase()`s the action because there it is a STANDALONE title line, not inline in a sentence — deliberately left unchanged. ### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 60

- NOTIF-PREF-EMPTY-COPY P3 FIXED & MERGED (commit edabecbf on master, fast-forward; deploying grantpipe-web). Live sweep of `/app/notifications` (web 3050) on a fresh org flagged a Preferences empty-state copy claim for source verification last tick; confirmed this tick as a real accuracy defect. The empty state read title "No notification preferences saved yet." / description "Delivery rules will appear here once your team starts receiving alerts." — but tracing the data path shows that causal claim is FALSE: `notifications/service.ts` `upsertNotificationPreference` creates a preference row ONLY when a user explicitly toggles email/in-app, and `reminders.ts` falls back to `DEFAULT_PREFERENCE = {emailEnabled:true, inAppEnabled:true}` for any type without a row. So receiving alerts does NOT create preference rows; preferences appear once a user CUSTOMIZES delivery, and by default every alert already goes to inbox + email. FIX (`notifications.tsx` line 315): rewrote the description to "Right now, every alert goes to your inbox and email. Your saved rules show up here." — states the actual default behavior in two short plain sentences. Title left unchanged. TDD: `notifications.test.tsx` "shows empty states…" — added a `getByText` assertion for the new description (red confirmed: old string no longer present). 20/20 pass; per-file coverage web `test:coverage` >=95% per file (pre-commit gate green). COPY: user-facing empty-state copy, so the required gate applies — passed `humanizer` (no AI tells; concrete, active, no copula-avoidance) + `third-grade-copy` (`evaluate_copy.py`: avg 8 words, max 10, FK grade 4.5 warning-level, no hard-gate failures). GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha), restored no generated-file churn (routeTree clean), pre-commit full web `test:coverage` green (~15m46s). Merged to master, worktree removed (rm -rf + prune, node_modules lock), deploying to production grantpipe-web. SWEEP NOTE (deeper gap, not actioned — would be new feature work, out of sweep scope): a brand-new user with zero preference rows has NO UI affordance to create one, because toggle buttons only render per existing row. Since defaults are all-on, the only meaningful action (opting OUT of a type) requires a row that the UI cannot create from empty. Flagged for a future feature wave, not a copy fix. Notifications inbox empty state ("New alerts will land here as grants, reports, and reminders change.") is accurate and left unchanged; 0 console errors on render.

### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 62

- CALENDAR-DEFAULT-TODAY P3 FIXED & MERGED (commit b79699c6 on master, fast-forward after a rebase onto an intervening billing merge; deploying grantpipe-web). Live sweep of `/app/calendar` (web 3050) on the sweep org: the month grid correctly marked the real today as "Today", but the "Selected day agenda" sidebar defaulted to the 1st of the month ("June 1, 2026 / No deadlines scheduled for June 2026.") instead of today. On load a user expects today selected. ROOT CAUSE (`calendar.tsx` useEffect ~line 267): when no day had items, the selected-date fallback was `firstDayWithItems ?? monthDays[0]?.dateKey ?? null` — `monthDays[0]` is the 1st, chosen even while viewing the current month. FIX: compute `monthIsCurrent = todayDateKey.startsWith(month)` and make the empty fallback `firstDayWithItems ?? (monthIsCurrent ? todayDateKey : monthDays[0]?.dateKey) ?? null`, preserving the firstDayWithItems priority and the 1st-of-month default for non-current months; added `todayDateKey` to the effect deps. TDD: `calendar.test.tsx` (system time mocked 2026-04-08) — "renders an empty calendar state" now asserts "April 8, 2026" is `data-calendar-day-state="selected"` and "April 1, 2026" is "clear" (was: April 1 "selected"); updated "highlights today's date with today class" to expect "selected" (today is now auto-selected, and selected state takes precedence over today in the dayState precedence chain — `isSelected > isToday`) plus an `aria-current="date"` assertion to prove today identity is still announced. The month-with-items test is unaffected (firstDayWithItems wins). 13/13 pass; web `test:coverage` >=95% per file (pre-commit gate green). COPY: no user-facing copy changed (behavior-only fix), gate N/A. GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha), routeTree clean (no churn), pre-commit full web `test:coverage` green (exit 0). Master had advanced with a billing merge mid-task, so rebased the feature branch onto master (clean, calendar-only) then fast-forwarded. Merged to master, worktree removed (rm -rf + prune, node_modules lock), deploying to production grantpipe-web. SWEEP NOTE: calendar otherwise healthy — month controls, day grid, today dot/aria-current, month-stats all correct; 0 console errors on render.

### UI/UX live walkthrough — Wave 73

- ACCOUNTING-ERROR-STATE-CANON P3 FIXED & MERGED (commit 813bc0d4 on master, fast-forward; deploying grantpipe-web). New consistency line after the loading-state sweep closed: ERROR-state rendering. Seven outliers render a bare `<p className="text-destructive ...">Failed to load X.</p>` with NO retry path (accounting/chart-of-accounts, accounting/ledger, payments/index, payments/$requestId ×3, grants/$grantId). FIX: `chart-of-accounts.tsx` + `ledger.tsx` import `Alert`; the bare error `<p>` is replaced with `<Alert variant="destructive" title="Unable to load accounts.|ledger.">` wrapping a `Try again` outline Button that calls `accountsQuery.refetch()` / `ledgerQuery.refetch()` — gives users a recovery action they previously lacked. `Button` inherits the pill geometry from @grantpipe/ui (Design Canon satisfied). COPY: the new strings ("Unable to load X." / "Try again") are reused already-shipped canonical sibling copy — simple, third-grade level, no new persuasive copy; gate satisfied by reuse. GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha), routeTree restored, heavy pre-commit hook green (exit 0, coverage >= 95% per file). Merged to master (ff), worktree removed (rm -rf + prune then branch delete, node_modules lock), deploying to production grantpipe-web. ### Wave 62 — log-gift date convention unified

- LOGGIFT-DATE-UTC FIXED & MERGED (commit 543395b0 on master, ff; deployed grantpipe-web version 0c1ff1a7). LogGiftDialog (apps/web/src/components/dialogs/log-gift-dialog.tsx:82) stored gift dates at noon UTC (`T12:00:00.000Z`) while the canonical DonationForm (donation-form.tsx:71) uses midnight UTC (`T00:00:00.000Z`). Both create donations, so the same gift date was persisted as two different instants depending on entry path — a real storage inconsistency affecting date equality/dedup/non-UTC consumers. FIX: noon -> midnight UTC, matching donation-form, with an explanatory comment. INVESTIGATION (why safe): all date-only display is UTC-locked (`timeZone: "UTC"` in entity-activity-section.tsx and shared `formatUtcCalendarDate`), so both conventions render the identical calendar date — the change is invisible to users, zero display risk. Confirmed the noon-vs-midnight split is intentional/mixed elsewhere (new-event, payments, subrecipients use noon; ~20 sites use midnight); did NOT mass-rewrite — scoped strictly to the donation-entry path that has a canonical midnight sibling. TDD: updated test expectation to T00:00:00 (RED), 25 log-gift tests GREEN. GATE: built web first (build-output gotcha), routeTree churn reverted, pre-commit web typecheck + test:coverage green. Review: lite agent APPROVE (verified donation-form convention + UTC-locked display). Worktree sweep-wave62 git-deregistered (leftover dir busy, harmless).
- SWEEP NOTE: two explorer passes this session found the codebase genuinely clean of easy objective defects (a11y broadly good; icon buttons have accessible names). Remaining opportunities trend toward larger refactors (repo-wide date-convention unification) or live-workflow UX testing rather than static one-liners.

### Wave 75 — AI award-document intake entry point restored

- AWARD-INTAKE-ENTRY-REGRESSION FIXED & MERGED (commit 3c55eabb on master, ff; deploying grantpipe-web). A live-walkthrough of the grant-creation flow surfaced a real FEATURE REGRESSION (not a styling nit): the AI award-document intake entry point was unreachable from the grants UI. The `<AwardIntakeEntry />` component (apps/web/src/components/document-extractions/award-intake-entry.tsx) — which lets entitled-plan users upload an award letter and have OpenRouter extract the grant fields — used to be rendered by the "Add grant" surface, but commit dcc4c111 refactored that surface into `apps/web/src/components/dialogs/new-grant-dialog.tsx` and silently DROPPED the component. The whole upload→extract→commit flow still existed end-to-end (hooks use-documents/use-document-extractions, the /award-intake/$extractionId route, the API endpoints) but NO surface rendered the entry point, so users could never start an extraction from the create-grant path. FIX (`new-grant-dialog.tsx`): imported `AwardIntakeEntry` and rendered `<AwardIntakeEntry compact />` at the top of step 1, followed by a divider ("or enter details by hand") above the manual name/funder/amount/status fields. The component self-gates: it calls `useSession()` unconditionally, then renders an upgrade notice when `!hasAwardDocumentIntake(effectivePlanTier)`, returns null when `!orgId`, and only mounts the hooks-heavy `AwardIntakeEnabledForm` subtree (useUploadDocument + useStartDocumentExtraction) when entitled — so wiring it into the dialog introduces no rules-of-hooks violation. On submit it uploads the file, starts the extraction, and navigates to /award-intake/$extractionId (which closes the dialog), exactly as the standalone page does. TDD: `new-grant-dialog.test.tsx` — added "offers the AI award document intake entry point on step 1" asserting both the "Create from award document" label and the "Start intake" button render under an entitled session; extended the default session mock with `orgId: "org-1"` + `effectivePlanTier: "growth"` (needed so the component passes its entitlement + orgId gates) and added mocks for useUploadDocument/useStartDocumentExtraction. RED confirmed (1 failed/33 passed), then GREEN: 34/34 pass. GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha), no routeTree churn, heavy pre-commit web typecheck + test:coverage green (exit 0). DESIGN CANON: the entry's submit Button inherits `rounded-full` from the @grantpipe/ui Button base class (button.tsx:8) — pills satisfied without a call-site override. Review: sonnet agent APPROVE (only flag was a pill-shape question on award-intake-entry.tsx:61, resolved by confirming the base Button defaults to rounded-full). COPY: the entry-point label "Create from award document" + "Start intake" are pre-existing in award-intake-entry.tsx (not newly authored this wave); no new persuasive copy introduced; gate satisfied. Merged to master (ff), worktree removed (rm -rf clean), deploying to production grantpipe-web. LIVE-TEST NOTE: an end-to-end OpenRouter extraction walkthrough against the local stack (web 3050 / wrangler api 5050 with OPENROUTER_API_KEY loaded into apps/api/.dev.vars) was attempted but blocked by Playwright MCP context detachment/disconnection; the wiring fix itself is unit-test-verified, and a live OpenRouter walkthrough remains a worthwhile follow-up once the browser tooling is stable. SWEEP NOTE: this is the first FEATURE-REACHABILITY regression caught this sweep (prior waves were empty/loading/error-state consistency) — worth a dedicated pass grepping for other components that exist + are tested but are no longer rendered by any route/surface after refactors.

### Wave 76 — AI award-intake live OpenRouter walkthrough: extraction→commit data-flow fixed

- AWARD-INTAKE-CANONICAL-FIELDS + JSONRECORD-STRINGIFIED-CHILD FIXED & MERGED (commits 182fd311 + 06730d4d on master, ff; deployed grantpipe-api-production version 5ba6506e). Completed the live OpenRouter walkthrough that Wave 75 left as a follow-up — and it surfaced two REAL end-to-end backend defects (not styling). Ran the actual extraction against the local fixture (.local/test-fixtures/award-letter.txt) with the real OPENROUTER*API_KEY. DEFECT 1 (data vocabulary mismatch): the model emitted free-form snake_case destinationField names (total_award_amount, project_start_date, report_due_date), often omitted the canonical funder/grant name keys, and reported money in dollars — while every commit consumer in document-extractions/service.ts reads canonical camelCase keys (funder.name, grant.name, grant.amountCents) in integer cents. Net effect: a reviewed award silently failed to create grant records. FIX: new deterministic persistence-time canonicalizer (document-extractions/canonical-fields.ts) mapping known aliases per entity to canonical fields and coercing dollar money to integer cents (conservative — plain integers treated as already-cents); wired into the extraction persistence loop; plus rewrote the OpenRouter system prompt to v2 teaching the canonical vocabulary + object-valued child records + integer cents, and constrained destinationEntityType to the known enum. DEFECT 2 (stringified child records): live output returned reporting_requirement / restriction_term values as a \_stringified* JSON object, but jsonRecord() only accepted real objects, so those child records were silently skipped at commit. FIX: jsonRecord() now parses a JSON-object string before falling back. LIVE VERIFICATION: a throwaway script driving the real OpenRouter call confirmed PASS — funder.name="The Riverstone Foundation", grant.name="Youth Literacy Initiative", grant.amountCents=25000000 (integer cents), grant.startDate=2026-01-01, grant.endDate=2026-12-31 all canonical post-canonicalizer (script removed before commit to keep the coverage surface clean). TDD: canonical-fields.test.ts (16 tests, incl. money-coercion edge branches) + openrouter.test.ts (+1 vocabulary/enum assertion) + service.test.ts (+3: stringified child commits, malformed-JSON-string skipped, restriction purposeStatement backfill). Per-file coverage on all three touched files clears the 95% gate (canonical-fields 100/96.42, openrouter 100/100, service 100/95.63). GATE: typecheck + lint clean; heavy pre-commit hook ran full @grantpipe/api test:coverage green on both commits (exit 0). Rebased onto current master (Wave 75 was a complementary WEB-side entry-point fix — no overlap with this API-side data-flow fix), ff-merged, pushed, worktree git-deregistered (leftover dir busy from a file handle, harmless), deployed grantpipe-api via Wrangler (award-intake queue consumer now runs the v2 prompt + canonicalizer + jsonRecord fix in production). SWEEP NOTE: this is the first sweep wave driven by a real live-LLM workflow rather than static inspection — it proved the upload→extract→review→commit pipeline now actually produces a committed grant. The web entry point (Wave 75) + this backend data-flow (Wave 76) together make AI award intake functional end-to-end.

### Wave 78 — divergent master-history reconciliation (origin force-rewind)

- HISTORY-RECONCILED & DEPLOYED (merge 4e4db1e5 + fix 8b14ab67 on master, pushed; deployed grantpipe-api 20012023, grantpipe-web 1764d2aa, grantpipe-site 2efe8a94). origin/master had been FORCE-REWOUND to a state missing local sweep Waves 54-77; local master was the newer, more complete, fully-gated line. Per user instruction "merge them", merged origin into local with `-X ours` (0 conflicts, additive). CRITICAL CATCH: the initial repo-wide `turbo typecheck` reported green but was a CACHED (FULL TURBO) result masking real breakage — a forced uncached `turbo typecheck --force` + `turbo test --force` exposed that `-X ours` had produced inconsistent "Frankenstein" files where origin's stale counterparts mixed with the local line: (1) import/service.ts had a verbatim-duplicated donation-row declaration block (amountCents/type/fundId/grantId redeclared → esbuild "already declared"); (2) org/service.ts carried origin's promoCode-on-selection logic + normalizePromoCode usage against the local line's intentionally `.strict()` billingSelectionSchema that excludes promoCode (test expects `.toThrow()`); (3) the superseded dead `ai-cs-widget.tsx`+test were resurrected (local line uses `ai-cs-support-widget.tsx`; the dead file referenced a non-existent `ai-cs` API route → TS7053); (4) stale pricing/content tests (site config 4-tier vs local 3-tier, marketed-capabilities plan count, homepage-content LAUNCH-promo leak) and an origin-only launch-offer brochure that contradicts the local line's removal of launch-promo positioning. FIX: restored every conflicting source file to the proven-gated local line (`git checkout goal/pristine-w77-backup -- <files>`), deleted the dead widget, dropped the brochure artifacts, restored the lockfile — making the working tree byte-identical to the backup line while the merge commit still joins the histories (divergence resolved). GATE: uncached `turbo typecheck --force` 6/6 green; uncached `turbo test --force` 6/6 green (rebuilt web first for the build-output.test.ts gotcha). Pushed master to origin (now reconciled, no longer divergent), deployed all three apps via Wrangler. Safety branch `goal/pristine-w77-backup` retained. LESSON: after any large merge, ALWAYS run typecheck/test with `--force` — turbo's content-hash cache can return a stale green from before the merge and hide merge-introduced breakage. NOTE: stale `.worktrees/pristine-w77` leftover dir is held by a file handle (Device busy); inert (no .git, unregistered), harmless, cleanup deferred.

### Wave 80 — AI award-intake review screen: human labels, formatted money, readable terms

- AWARD-INTAKE-REVIEW-DISPLAY FIXED & MERGED (commit 15ff2f3a on master, ff; deployed grantpipe-web version e5e9cb44). First wave driven by a real LIVE OpenRouter E2E walkthrough on an ALTERNATE local port (web 3070 / wrangler api 5070, OPENROUTER_API_KEY from apps/api/.dev.vars) — port 3050 was held by another agent's process (not killed, per process-safety; ran on 3070 instead). Uploaded the .local/test-fixtures/award-letter.txt fixture through the restored new-grant-dialog entry point, the real extraction returned accurate fields (funder "The Riverstone Foundation", grant "Youth Literacy Initiative", $250,000, dates, reporting + restriction terms, 0 console errors) — confirming Waves 75+76 work end-to-end live. But viewing the /award-intake/$extractionId REVIEW screen (extraction-review.tsx) with a critical eye surfaced a real TASTE/USABILITY defect on the marquee AI surface: it rendered raw internals to end users. (1) Field labels were code paths — `grant.amountCents`, `funder.name`, `grant.startDate`; (2) the award amount showed raw cents `25000000` instead of currency; (3) reporting/restriction values were dumped as raw JSON blobs (`{"reportType":"...","dueDate":"..."}`); (4) citations showed a literal `Page ?:` when the model gave no page number. For software sold to time-poor EDs (CLAUDE.md: "the consultant is the competitor; every UI must be self-evident"), this failed the bar. FIX (display-only, extraction-review.tsx): FIELD_LABELS map + humanizeKey fallback → friendly labels ("Award amount", "Start date", "Reporting requirement", "Restriction term"); formatAmountCents via Intl.NumberFormat USD → `$250,000.00`; new `FieldValue` component renders plain-object values as a readable `<dl>` of humanized key/value pairs instead of JSON; citation drops the "Page N:" prefix (using `!= null` so page 0 stays valid) when pageNumber is absent. aria-labels (keyed on fieldKey) and the entire edit/save/map/commit round-trip are intentionally UNCHANGED — the edit textbox still holds the literal JSON value because it must round-trip as JSON. TDD: extraction-review.test.tsx — updated the object-values/missing-page test to assert readable key/value pairs + no-"Page ?" + no raw-JSON, added "renders friendly labels, formatted money, and numbered citations" (asserts "Award amount" label not "grant.amountCents", "$250,000.00" not "25000000", "Page 2:"). RED then GREEN: 16/16 pass; per-file coverage 100% stmts/funcs/lines, 98.57% branch (the 2 uncovered branches pre-existing, not introduced) — clears 95% gate. GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha — fresh worktree had no dist; first commit attempt was correctly BLOCKED by the pre-commit hook on missing dist, rebuilt, re-committed green); heavy pre-commit hook ran full @grantpipe/web typecheck + test:coverage green (174 files / 3977 tests, exit 0, ~16m). LIVE VISUAL VERIFY: served the worktree build on port 3071 against the same extraction id — screenshot confirmed the transformed review screen (friendly labels, $250,000.00, readable reporting/restriction term rows, "Page 1:"), 0 console errors. REVIEW: sonnet lite agent — NO BLOCKERS; three low-severity future-schema notes (nested-object leaves would render JSON; humanizeKey lowercases acronyms; FIELD_LABELS is global not section-scoped) all masked by today's one-level scalar-leaf field set, explicitly APPROVED merge. Merged to master (ff), pushed, branch deleted + worktree git-deregistered (leftover dir busy from the port-3071 dev server file handle, harmless), deployed grantpipe-web via Wrangler. SWEEP NOTE: static scouting had hit diminishing returns (three prior candidates dissolved under scrutiny, navigation audit clean) — this defect was only visible by actually RUNNING the marquee workflow live and looking at the output with taste. Confirms the goal's premise that live E2E finds what static inspection cannot.

### Wave 81 — non-critical afterMain widgets isolated (SilentErrorBoundary)

- AFTERMAIN-WIDGET-RESILIENCE FIXED & MERGED (commit b6f0c601 on master, ff; deployed grantpipe-web version c7375ec0). Found via LIVE E2E this session (fresh signup→onboarding→landed on /app/grants): the authenticated shell white-screened into the RouteErrorBoundary because the lazy `<AiCsSupportWidget>` (external `@ventora/ai-cs` package) dynamic import 500'd. Root cause class: `_authenticated.tsx` afterMain wrapped the lazy widget only in `<Suspense fallback={null}>` — but Suspense does NOT catch errors, so a failed chunk fetch (transient network OR a stale chunk hash after a new deploy — a common real-world prod event) propagated to the route-level error boundary and took down the ENTIRE authenticated app. A non-essential support widget must never be able to crash the app. FIX: new `apps/web/src/components/silent-error-boundary.tsx` (`SilentErrorBoundary` class — getDerivedStateFromError + componentDidCatch reporting via captureReactBoundaryError wrapped in try/catch, renders `fallback ?? null` on error; mirrors error-boundary.tsx). Wrapped BOTH afterMain widgets — `<AiCsSupportWidget>` (inside its existing Suspense) and `<CrmFeedbackWidget>` (external script loader) — each in its own SilentErrorBoundary with distinct source tags ("ai-cs-support-widget"/"crm-feedback-widget") for Sentry triage, so one failing widget hides only itself. TDD: silent-error-boundary.test.tsx (7 tests, all branches, 100% stmt/branch/func/lines). GATE: web typecheck + full test:coverage green (exit 0); built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha). No routeTree churn. No user-facing copy (widgets render nothing on failure) so copy gate N/A. Sub-agent (sonnet) implemented in worktree → orchestrator reviewed diff (clean) → ff-merged → worktree removed → pushed → deployed grantpipe-web. ENV NOTE: this checkout was also missing the `@ventora/ai-cs@0.6.0` install (declared in apps/web/package.json, resolvable from the private Ventora registry via ~/.npmrc token); a `pnpm install` fetched it — that was a local-checkout gap, not a product defect (unit tests mock the package). SWEEP NOTE: confirms again that live E2E surfaces resilience bugs static inspection cannot — and flags a follow-up class: audit other lazy()/dynamic-import or external-script surfaces for the same Suspense-without-error-boundary gap.

### Wave 82 — AI award-intake commit: accepted-date data loss + commit-gate clarity

- AWARD-INTAKE-COMMIT-DATELOSS + COMMIT-GATE FIXED & MERGED (commit ee3781ef, merge 66b3bb12 on master, no-ff; deployed grantpipe-web version 84a95f46). Driven by a real live OpenRouter E2E walkthrough (upload→extract→review→commit creating an actual grant row), then DB-verified the committed grant. Two real defects on the marquee award-intake commit path (extraction-review.tsx). DEFECT 1 (DATA LOSS): `extractedFieldValue` returned only `?.normalizedValueJson` — but the model emits normalized values for AMOUNTS only, leaving date/string fields un-normalized (null). Net: accepted, 100%-confidence grant start/end dates were silently dropped from the committed grant (DB row confirmed start_date=null/end_date=null despite accepted dates). FIX: fall back to `?? field?.valueJson`, mirroring the server's own `normalizedValueJson ?? valueJson` and the existing `extractedGrantName` pattern. The `[accepted,edited,mapped_existing]` filter is unchanged so rejected/deferred fields never contribute values. DEFECT 2 (premature/raw commit errors): the Commit button enabled before required fields were reviewed, and a failed commit surfaced the raw `review_incomplete` code to the user. FIX: added `isBlockingCommitField` (exact parity with server `findBlockingCommitFields`: `(required||confidence<70) && !isReviewedFieldStatus` over `[accepted,edited,rejected,deferred,mapped_existing]`), gated `canCommit` on `unreviewedBlockingCount===0`, added a plain-language guidance hint under the button, and a `commitErrorMessage` mapper (review_incomplete / missing_approved_funder_name / grant_funder_mismatch → third-grade guidance; unknown → raw message as before). TDD: 5 new tests (date-from-valueJson commit, commit-gate-blocked + hint, review_incomplete plain-language, +2 parametrized error-code maps); touched file 100% stmts/lines, 98.7% branch (>=95 gate). GATE: built web first (build-output.test.ts gotcha); typecheck+lint clean; full web suite 4012 passed (1 pre-existing dist-artifact failure, unrelated); heavy pre-commit hook green (exit 0). REVIEW: sonnet agent confirmed exact server-contract parity — the reviewed-status asymmetry (rejected/deferred count as reviewed for GATING but excluded from VALUES) is intentional and mirrors server `isReviewedFieldStatus` vs `isAcceptedCommitStatus`; no `??`-leaks-rejected-value risk. Merged to master (no-ff), worktree removed + branch deleted, deployed grantpipe-web. FOLLOW-UP FLAGGED: OpenRouter model is pinned to `google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview` while stable `google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite` exists — latent prod-stability risk, candidate for a future wave.
