### Wave 144 (2026-06-12) — DONE — activity true-empty filter chrome + notifications per-row mark-read scoping

Two distinct defects found auditing the activity log and notifications routes (the non-list top-level routes W143 deferred):

- **A1 — `/activity` true-empty filter chrome (same class as W141/W143):** the entity-type `<Select>` + From/To date-range `<Input>` toolbar (`<section className="space-y-3">`) rendered _above_ the first-run `TeachAndActEmptyState` even with zero log entries and no active filter — nothing to filter, and it undercut the teaching empty state. Gated behind `const hasActivityFilterChrome = entries.length > 0 || hasActiveFilters;` and wrapped the toolbar `{hasActivityFilterChrome ? ( … ) : null}`. The toolbar returns the moment any entry exists or a filter is active (so the filter-active "no matches" path is preserved). The "Activity log" heading stays unconditional. Source-confirmed before fixing; TDD (failing true-empty chrome-hidden test first).
- **C1 — `/notifications` per-row "Mark read" disable keyed off shared mutation variables:** each row's disable read `markRead.isPending && markRead.variables === notification.id`. `markRead.variables` only ever holds the **last-fired** id, so rapidly marking row A then row B re-enabled A's button while A's request was still in flight (and could flicker the wrong row's spinner). Replaced with a local `const [pendingReadIds, setPendingReadIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set())`: the id is added before `mutate` and removed in an `onSettled` callback, so each row keeps its own isolated pending state. The single global `markAllRead` button (correctly using `markAllRead.isPending`) was left unchanged.

**Bundle guard:** the two route edits nudged the initial app entry chunk to 852,091 bytes, just past the 852,000 `build-output.test.ts` ratchet. Bumped the guard to 854,000 per its own documented convention (entry stays the only growth; the modulepreload code-split guard is unchanged and still passes — all `_authenticated` route code remains split out).

**TDD/coverage:** added cross-row isolation tests (mark A then B; assert A stays disabled until its own `onSettled`), a re-enable-after-`onSettled` test, and a true-empty "chrome hidden" test. Updated the `markRead.mutate` call-args assertion to include `onSettled: expect.any(Function)`. Per-file coverage: activity 100/96.77/100/100, notifications 100/100/100/100. **Full `@grantpipe/web` suite re-run with the real gate (789s): 4140/4141 pass — the lone failure was the entry-chunk guard above, fixed and re-confirmed green; "Web source coverage is >= 95% per file" holds (husky pre-commit again returned a fast green — not trusted; re-ran the gate directly per the standing lesson).**

### Wave 146 (2026-06-12) — DONE — settings per-row mutation scoping (CLASS-3, flagged in W145 audit)

On the team + portal-access settings tables, each row's action button disabled on a **shared** mutation's `.isPending`, so an in-flight action on one row visibly greyed out the buttons on **every** row — the same class as the W144 notifications fix, and an inconsistency a buyer's eye catches (clicking row A's "Promote" should not disable row B's "Remove"). Fixed by scoping each per-row button to its own row via the mutation's `.variables`, matching the **existing repo convention** already used in `accounting/recurring.tsx` (`deleteMutation.variables === template.id`) and `accounting/bank/$bankAccountId.tsx` (`ignoreMutation.variables?.bankTransactionId === txn.id`) — no new `useState`/Set introduced (the awaited single-select role changes + dialog-gated removes here have no rapid-concurrent path, unlike W144 notifications which needed a Set).

- **`settings.tsx`** team rows — `updateMember` handles both role-change and remove (`variables.memberId`); both the "Promote/Change" outline button and "Remove" destructive button now gate on `updateMember.isPending && updateMember.variables?.memberId === member.id`.
- **`settings.team.tsx`** — the dedicated team route's per-row "Remove" gated the same way.
- **`settings.portal-access.tsx`** — reviewer "Remove" → `deleteReviewer.variables === reviewer.id`. Also fixed the same-class sibling in the same file the audit hadn't named: per-session-row "Extend" (`extendSession.variables?.id === session.id`) and "Revoke" (`revokeSession.variables === session.id`) buttons, which shared one mutation across multiple session rows.

ConfirmDialog `isPending` guards (each acts on a single selected id) left unchanged — correct as-is.

### Wave 163 (2026-06-13) — DONE — Event attendee donations left the donor's giving totals stale

CLASS instance: **mutation that doesn't invalidate the queries it affects** (same class as recent commits "refresh fund views after allocation/expense mutations"). In `use-events.ts`, `useCreateAttendeeDonation` and `useLinkAttendeeDonation` only called `invalidateEvent()` (`["event", id]`, `["events"]`, `["volunteer-hours"]`). But logging/linking a donation through the event attendee panel changes the contact's giving history and org-wide donor stats — yet `["donations", contactId]`, `["contact", contactId]`, `["donor-stats"]`, `["retention-stats"]` were never invalidated. After the action, the donor's contact page and the donors dashboard showed stale lifetime-giving, last-gift date, and donation counts until the cache expired. The sibling donor-domain mutations (`useCreateDonation`/`useUpdateDonation`/`useDeleteDonation` in `use-donors.ts`) all invalidate those four keys — proving the attendee hooks were the anomaly.

**Fix:** added an optional `contactId` param to both hooks; on success they still invalidate the event, and when `contactId` is present also invalidate the four contact-giving keys via a new `invalidateContactGiving` helper. The event detail route (`events/$eventId.tsx`) threads `attendee.contactId` into both hooks.

**TDD/coverage:** RED-first — added four hook tests (contactId-present asserts all four giving keys; contactId-absent asserts event-only, `donor-stats` not called) for each hook; confirmed 2 failing, then GREEN. Added `contactId` to the `eventWithAttendee` route fixture and a route assertion that the hook is constructed with `("evt-test-1", "att-1", "contact-1")`, covering the truthy branch. **Full `@grantpipe/web` gate re-run independently after `vite build`: build + typecheck clean, coverage exit 0, `use-events.ts` 100% per file, overall ≥95% per touched file** (pre-commit not trusted).

### Wave 162 (2026-06-13) — DONE — Indirect cost rule "Remove" deleted instantly with no confirmation (destructive, no undo)

CLASS instance: **destructive action with no confirmation**. In the payment-request detail view (`payments/$requestId.tsx`), the per-row "Remove" button in the `IndirectCostPanel` called `mutations.deleteIndirectRule.mutateAsync(rule.id)` directly on first click — a mis-click silently dropped an indirect cost rule with no undo. The two sibling destructive actions in the same file (removeLine, removePayment) already gate behind `ConfirmDialog`; this row did not.

**Fix:** added `confirmRemoveRuleId` state; the row "Remove" now only opens a `ConfirmDialog` ("Remove indirect cost rule?" / "This cannot be undone."). Confirm reads the id into a local, clears state, then runs the mutation with `.catch` surfacing `rowError`. Matches the existing confirm pattern in the file. Also bumped the entry-chunk creep guard in `build-output.test.ts` `854_000 → 856_000`: cumulative shared-chunk growth on master had already pushed the entry past the prior guard (854,285 on master vs 854,093 here); the code-split route change itself did not grow the entry.

**TDD/coverage:** replaced the old direct-delete tests with four covering the confirm flow (first click only opens the dialog; cancel leaves the rule; delete after confirm calls `mutateAsync` with "rule-1"; error after confirm surfaces the message) plus the non-Error fallback path. All 93 file tests pass. **Full `@grantpipe/web` gate re-run independently after `vite build`: build + typecheck clean, coverage ≥95% per touched file (`$requestId.tsx` 99.91%), exit 0** (pre-commit not trusted).

### Wave 160 (2026-06-13) — DONE — Funder contact delete fired instantly with no confirmation (destructive, no undo)

New CLASS instance: **unguarded destructive action**. On the funder detail page (`funders/$funderId.tsx`), the per-contact "Delete contact {name}" button called the delete mutation directly on the first click — no confirmation, no undo. Every other delete in the app (funder, fund, grant, …) routes through a confirm dialog; the program-officer delete was the lone exception, and it is one misclick from silently removing a funder's primary contact.

**Fix:** wrapped the contact delete in a controlled confirmation `Dialog` that mirrors the existing funder-delete flow. Added `deletingContactId` state + a shared `closeDeleteContact` handler; the trigger now only opens the dialog (`setDeletingContactId(contact.id)`), and `deleteContact.mutateAsync` runs only after the user confirms. Cancel and the dialog's `onOpenChange` both close without deleting (one shared close fn so coverage stays simple). Controlled pattern (plain trigger + `open`/`onOpenChange`) chosen over `DialogTrigger asChild` so the mock-renders-everything test harness has no untestable ternary branch.

**TDD/coverage:** added two RED tests in `$funderId.test.tsx` — (1) first click opens the dialog without calling `deleteContact` (asserts `data-open` flips false→true), (2) Cancel closes without deleting — confirmed failing, then implemented to GREEN. Updated the 4 existing contact-delete tests + 3 funder-delete confirm clicks to scope the confirm "Delete"/"Cancel" via `within(<dialog wrapper>)` helpers, since the mocked `Dialog` renders all bodies unconditionally and the two dialogs share button labels. Also fixed the sibling `grants-funds-funders-pages.test.tsx`: three `getByText(name)` → `getByText(name, { selector: "p" })` (disambiguate the card `<p>` from the new dialog `<strong>`), and the combined update+delete flow now clicks both the contact- and funder-scoped confirm Delete buttons. Per-file coverage `$funderId.tsx` 99.81/97.72/100/99.81 (lone uncovered line 483 is the pre-existing `canEdit` else-branch, >95%). **Full `@grantpipe/web` gate re-run independently after `vite build`: typecheck clean, all tests pass, coverage ≥95% per touched file, exit 0** (pre-commit not trusted; the on-commit affected-gate also passed in 10m36s).

### Wave 159 (2026-06-13) — DONE — DataTable sort buttons exposed camelCase column ids to screen readers (app-wide a11y)

New defect class: **accessible-name leakage**. During the live Donors-page E2E walk, every sortable column header's button announced the raw internal field key instead of the visible label — "Sort by totalGivingCents", "Sort by pipelineStage", "Sort by lastDonationDate", "Sort by createdAt", etc. Traced to the shared `packages/ui/src/components/data-table.tsx`, so the defect affected **every sortable table in the app** (donors, grants, funders, reports, accounting, …), not one page. The visible text was already correct (rendered via `flexRender(columnDef.header)`); only the `aria-label` was wrong, so it was invisible to sighted QA and only surfaced to screen-reader users.

**Fix:** the sort `aria-label` now derives from `columnDef.header` when it is a string (the human label), falling back to `column.id` only for non-string (render-function) headers — mirroring the existing line-210 column-visibility-menu helper. Three template literals (asc/desc/clear) updated to use the resolved `sortName`.

**TDD/coverage:** added a RED test in `data-table.test.tsx` asserting a camelCase-keyed column (`accessorKey: "totalGivingCents"`, `header: "Total Giving"`) exposes "Sort by Total Giving" and never `/totalGivingCents/`; confirmed it failed, then implemented to GREEN. Existing case-insensitive sort tests still pass; the existing "non-string header falls back to id" test still passes. Updated `donors/index.test.tsx` (the only test asserting an exact `Sort by …` string) from "Sort by name" → "Sort by Name". No other test files referenced camelCase sort labels (docs/evidence YAML do, but they are not executed). Full per-file gate re-run in-worktree for `@grantpipe/ui` + `@grantpipe/web` after `vite build`: all pass (the lone `build-output.test.ts` failure was the fresh-worktree no-dist condition, resolved by building; re-ran green), ≥95% per touched file, `tsc` clean. Pre-commit affected-gate re-ran on commit (not trusted; verified independently).

### Wave 158 (2026-06-13) — DONE — refresh fund views after allocation/expense mutations

New defect class: **cross-entity stale cache** (distinct from the now-exhausted dashboard-overview staleness class). A haiku audit confirmed the dashboard-overview class is closed — every dashboard-feeding mutation now invalidates `["dashboard-overview"]`. A second audit on the cross-entity class found a real holdout in grant↔fund allocations/expenses.

Allocations and expenses each carry a `fundId` (`packages/db/src/schema/grants.ts`) and are rendered on the fund detail page (`apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/funds/$fundId.tsx`): `fund.grantAllocations`, `fund.expenses`, and the balance summary (`allocatedTotalCents` / `expenseTotalCents`, computed by `getFund()` in `apps/api/src/domains/grants/fund.service.ts`). But `useAllocationMutations` and `useExpenseMutations` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-grants.ts`) invalidated only the grant keys via `invalidateGrant` — never the fund's `["fund", id]` detail or `["funds"]` list. Creating, editing, deleting, or moving an allocation/expense left the affected fund page stale until manual refresh.

**Fix:** new `invalidateGrantAndFunds` helper — calls `invalidateGrant`, then invalidates the `["funds"]` list and the `["fund"]` detail prefix (TanStack prefix-matches `["fund", anyId]`, covering create/update/delete _and_ moves between funds without needing the old fundId). Wired into all six allocation/expense `onSuccess` handlers.

**TDD/coverage:** extended the existing allocation and expense specs in `use-grants.test.ts` — after the delete `onSuccess`, asserted `["fund"]` and `["funds"]` invalidation. Confirmed RED (neither key seen), then GREEN. Full `@grantpipe/web` gate re-run in-worktree after `vite build`: coverage ≥95% per file (`use-grants.ts` 99.74/98.9/100; the 69-70 gap is pre-existing), `verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs` exit 0, `tsc --noEmit` clean (pre-commit not trusted).

### Wave 157 (2026-06-13) — DONE — refresh dashboard overview after board report generation

Continued the **stale-data-after-mutation** class. A targeted haiku audit + direct code read found one more `invalidateOverview` holdout: `useGenerateBoardReport` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-reports.ts`) invalidated only `["reports"]`. The dashboard overview derives a `boardReportFreshness` panel from the latest `generatedReports` row of type `board`/status `ready` (`apps/api/src/domains/overview/service.ts` lines 753–757, 861–875). Board generation is **synchronous** — `storeArtifact` (`apps/api/src/domains/compliance/service.ts`) writes the `pending` row then the `ready` row in the same request — so a successful generate changes that panel immediately, but the dashboard kept showing the prior board-report freshness until a manual refresh. **Fix:** import `invalidateOverview` and call it alongside `invalidateReports` in `useGenerateBoardReport.onSuccess`.

### Wave 155 (2026-06-13) — DONE — gate funder-contact Save buttons against double-submit

The W154 ledger flagged the funder detail page's two contact "Save contact" buttons as a follow-up: both the add-contact dialog form (`funders/$funderId.tsx`) and the inline per-row edit form fire `createContact`/`updateContact` `mutateAsync` with **no `disabled` prop**, so a double click could submit twice. ### Wave 154 (2026-06-13) — DONE — gate the last three required-name Save buttons (programs create + edit, custom-field create)

A haiku Explore audit of the remaining `type="submit"` dialogs (after W152/W153 closed funder + fund) found **three controlled create/edit dialogs** whose primary Save button still gated only on the mutation pending state, not on the required name being non-empty — the last holdouts vs the fund/funder/grant/event convention:

- `programs/index.tsx` "Save program" (create) and `programs/$programId.tsx` "Save changes" (edit): both had `required` on the name input, so HTML5 blocked an empty submit, but the button stayed visually active with an empty name — a weaker affordance than the disabled-button pattern used everywhere else.
- `custom-fields-settings-section.tsx` "Save custom field" (create): **no native `required`** — clickable into a raw schema validation error, the strongest of the three.

Fix: add `|| <name>.trim().length === 0` to each existing pending gate (`formData.name` / `editFormData.name` / `name`). For custom fields the empty-name schema-error path is now reachable only via direct form submission; its existing "surfaces validation errors" test was rewritten to `fireEvent.submit(form)` (the button is disabled while empty).

Triaged as **non-defects this wave** (different rationale, deferred): `restriction-term-form.tsx` and the funder-contact create/edit forms in `funders/$funderId.tsx` are uncontrolled (FormData + native `required` / in-handler empty-name guard) — they have no controlled name state to gate `disabled` on at render; the funder-contact Save buttons do lack a _pending_ gate (double-submit risk) and are a candidate for a follow-up wave.

### Wave 153 (2026-06-13) — DONE — gate the "Save fund" button in the funds-list create dialog (double-submit + inconsistent affordance)

Defect found continuing the live create-modal walk after W152. The funds-list create dialog (`apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/funds/index.tsx`) "Save fund" button had **no `disabled` prop** — it was clickable with an empty name and stayed live during the in-flight create, allowing a double-submit. Every sibling create dialog (grant/funder/event, plus W152's inline funder) gates Save on pending-or-empty-name; the fund dialog was the lone holdout, so the affordance was inconsistent and the only empty-name feedback was the post-submit "Fund name is required." error.

The defensive empty-name guard in `handleSubmit` stays (now reachable only via direct form submission) and is covered by a test that submits the form element directly. Simplified the name check to a plain-string trim (no nullish branch) since `formData.name` is always a string — that lifted `funds/index.tsx` branch coverage from 94.18 → **95.65%**, so the file's `94.18` entry was **dropped from `verify-coverage-thresholds.mjs`** and it is now held to the full 95 on all four metrics (98.34/95.65/96.87/98.34).

### Wave 152 (2026-06-13) — DONE — inline "Add a funder" dialog: humane validation messages + gated Save button

Defect found during the live walk of the Create-grant flow. The inline `NewFunderInlineDialog` (`apps/web/src/components/dialogs/new-funder-inline-dialog.tsx`, nested in the new-grant flow so a brand-new org can add a funder without losing its half-filled grant) had two rough edges: (1) it leaked **raw Zod messages** — submitting an empty name surfaced "Too small: expected string to have >=1 characters", and a bad website surfaced "Invalid url"; (2) the **"Save funder" button was not gated** — clickable with an empty name, so the only feedback was the raw error alert. Both are below the bar for software we sell to big clients.

Fix (two halves): (a) `packages/shared` `createFunderSchema` now carries human messages — `name` `.min(1, "Enter a funder name.")` / `.max(200, "Funder name must be 200 characters or fewer.")`, `website` `z.url("Enter a valid website URL, including https://")`; (b) the dialog's Save button is now `disabled={createFunder.isPending || name.trim().length === 0}`, matching the parent grant modal's gating. Added 3 schema tests (assert the exact human strings, and that they are NOT the raw Zod text) and a dedicated `new-funder-inline-dialog.test.tsx` (8 tests; the file reaches 100 stmts/funcs/lines, 95 branch — the lone uncovered branch is the unreachable `?? "Unable to save funder."` parse-issue fallback). Rewrote the one existing `new-grant-dialog` test that clicked Save with an empty name (it asserted the old raw-error behavior) to assert the disabled-until-named behavior instead.

The mutation payload is an ordered `itemIds[]` (no single row id), so added a `reorderingItemId` state set before the mutation and cleared in `finally`; each row's disable now requires `isPending && reorderingItemId === item.id`.
- **CLASS-3 true-empty filter chrome** — `subrecipients/index.tsx`: the search + Status/Risk/Overdue/Findings toolbar rendered above the first-run empty state for brand-new orgs (zero rows, no active filter). Gated behind `hasSubrecipientListChrome = rows.length > 0 || hasActiveFilters`, matching the funders/programs convention. Reconciled one pre-existing test: clearing filters in a zero-row org now correctly transitions filtered-empty → true-empty (toolbar unmounts), so the test asserts that transition.
- **CLASS-4 raw-anchor basepath bypass** — audited, **zero findings** across these surfaces (in-app empty-state links route through the `RouterEmptyStateLink` provider).

### Wave 148 (2026-06-12) — DONE — confirmation gate on 7 immediate-delete destructive actions (new CLASS-4) + latent typecheck cleanup

A new source-greppable class surfaced auditing destructive actions (delete/remove/revoke) across `apps/web/src`: **irreversible row/list buttons that fire their mutation immediately on click with no confirmation step.** Of 28 destructive call sites, 21 already used the repo's safe pattern (a `ConfirmDialog`/`AlertDialog` or a `setXToDelete` dialog gate); **7 did not** — one stray click destroyed data. Notable because `$grantId.tsx` already had a `ConfirmDialog` for metric _entries_, so the gap was internally inconsistent.

- **`grants/$grantId.tsx`** — Delete buttons for allocation, expense, metric, requirement, and closeout item.
- **`payments/$requestId.tsx`** — Remove buttons for budget line items and payments.

Each trigger button now only sets a `useState<string | null>` confirm-target id; the actual `mutateAsync` (and its existing error-capture / `runGrantAction`/`runRowAction` wrapper) moved into a `ConfirmDialog`'s `onConfirm`, titled per action ("Delete allocation?" … "Remove payment?") with "This cannot be undone." Each dialog is **conditionally rendered** (`{confirmXId !== null ? <ConfirmDialog open …/> : null}`) so the target id narrows to a non-null `string` inside the closure — no dead `if (!id) return` branch, which let the `$grantId.tsx` legacy coverage baseline stay at its original 94.97% branches rather than being lowered (the sub-agent's first pass had weakened it to 94.5 to absorb the guard branches — reverted).

**Latent typecheck reds cleared (pre-existing, surfaced by an independent `tsc` run the false-green pre-commit never did):** W144's `notifications.test.tsx` indexed-access (`buttons[0]` → `buttons[0]!`), W147's `help.test.tsx` old 2-arg `vi.fn<[], T>()` → `vi.fn<() => T>()`, a duplicated `DialogClose`/`DialogFooter` mock key in `grants-funds-funders-pages.test.tsx`, and ~17 `within(el.closest('[data-testid="dialog-content"]')!)` Element-vs-HTMLElement errors (fixed with the `.closest<HTMLElement>(…)` generic). `pnpm --filter @grantpipe/web typecheck` now reports **0 errors** (it was red on master before this wave).

**TDD/coverage:** for each of the 7 actions, a test asserts the trigger click does NOT immediately delete — a confirm dialog appears and the mutation fires only after its confirm button; existing tests that clicked the old immediate-delete button were remediated to trigger→confirm. **Full `@grantpipe/web` gate re-run in-worktree: 178 files / 4158 tests pass, "Web source coverage is >= 95% per file, except documented legacy baselines", COV_EXIT=0; build clean (entry chunk 853,110 < 854,000 ratchet, no bump).** Pre-commit not trusted; gate + typecheck run directly per standing lesson.

### Wave 217 (2026-06-14) — DONE (foundation) — local stack repaired; live visual phase unblocked + code-split class scoped

The renewed `/goal` re-emphasized the **live visual/interaction phase** (real local servers, screenshots of every screen/modal, real E2E workflows). That phase was blocked because **the local stack did not actually run**:

1. **Missing `apps/api/.dev.vars`** — gitignored, absent on this machine, so `wrangler dev` had no `DATABASE_URL`/`BETTER_AUTH_SECRET`. Recreated from `.dev.vars.example` pointing at the **local docker Postgres** (`postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:55439/grantpipe`) so sweeps don't mutate production Neon and `createDbHandle` uses node-postgres.
2. **Stale global-npm wrangler squatting on :5050** — an earlier `pnpm --filter @grantpipe/api dev` had launched the _global_ npm-installed `wrangler` workerd (from the roaming npm prefix, not `node_modules/`) before `.dev.vars` existed; it kept the port with `c.env.DATABASE_URL` **undefined**, so every DB route 500'd (`isLocalDatabaseUrl` → `new URL(undefined)` → "Invalid URL string.") while a second correct workerd was _also_ bound to 5050. Symptom was `/api/health` 200 but `/api/auth/better/get-session` 500. Diagnosed by temporarily catching in the db-init middleware and returning the error as JSON (wrangler suppresses per-request console when stdout is non-TTY). Killed all grantpipe workerd/wrangler procs, freed 5050, started one clean repo wrangler. Diagnostic instrumentation fully reverted — `app.ts` is back to a no-op diff.

Brought up: `pnpm db:local:start` → migrate → `seed-demo.ts` (demo@grantpipe.com / Demo2026!, org "Heartland Senior Services", rich realistic data). Logged in via Playwright; dashboard + grants render cleanly. Saved setup + gotcha to durable memory (`reference_local-stack-setup`).

**Visual spot-checks (legible viewport):** Dashboard and Grants Portfolio both sell-ready — pill segmented controls, pill filter dropdowns, pill primary buttons, semantic status badges, clean tables, generous whitespace. 0 console errors on both. No defects called this pass (more surfaces pending).

**Concrete systemic finding — CODE-SPLIT-01 (OPEN, next wave):** all 61 dev-console warnings on every authenticated page are the same TanStack Router warning — each route file does `export function XPage()` at the bottom (consumed by its co-located `.test.tsx`) alongside `export const Route`, which **disables code-splitting** for that route (real production initial-bundle cost) and floods the console. ~50 route files. Clean fix = extract each page component into a sibling module (e.g. `dashboard.page.tsx`) so the route file exports only `Route`; tests re-import from the page module. This is the "code-split refactor deferred" item (W216). It is a large, test-touching, coverage-/bundle-ratchet-sensitive refactor — do it as a dedicated sub-agent-driven wave on a worktree, not bundled into a discovery pass. No master change required for it yet.

No code change merged this wave (stack repair + diagnosis only; `.dev.vars` is gitignored). NEXT: (a) execute CODE-SPLIT-01 as its own wave; (b) continue the live visual walk — every remaining authenticated surface, then every create/edit modal, command palette ⌘K, and empty/error/loading states — capturing screenshots and running real create→edit→delete workflows on the now-working local stack.

### Wave 218 (2026-06-14) — DONE — event detail field parity (DEFECT-2), delete-cache 404 (DEFECT-3), header sizing (DEFECT-1) + first CODE-SPLIT-01 fix (eventId)

Live E2E walking of the working local stack (W217) surfaced three concrete `apps/web` defects, all source-verified and batched into one worktree (`worktree-event-detail-and-header-fixes`, rebased onto master, ff-merged):

- **DEFECT-2 — event detail Overview could not edit date/location/revenue-goal/description.** The create dialog (`new-event-dialog.tsx`) persisted all four, the DB schema (`events.ts`), validator (`updateEventSchema`), and service (`updateEvent`) all supported them, but the detail page (`events/$eventId.tsx`) only rendered name + type — so after creation those fields were permanently uneditable (a create-vs-detail field-parity gap). Added Date/Location/Revenue-goal/Description inputs wired to local override state, and expanded the Save payload (date → `T12:00:00.000Z` ISO like the create dialog, revenue via `centsFromInput`, cleared fields → `null`).
- **DEFECT-3 — deleting an event threw a 404 to console/Sentry.** `deleteEvent.onSuccess` invalidated `["event", id]`, so the still-mounted detail route refetched the soft-deleted row. New `invalidateAfterEventDelete` helper `removeQueries(["event", id])` instead and only invalidates the list + volunteer-hours views.
- **DEFECT-1 — Help + Subrecipients (list & detail) PageHeaders used the default 30px title** instead of the canonical 24px `variant="workbench"` (41 other files use workbench). Added the prop to all three.
- **CODE-SPLIT-01 (first concrete fix):** `$eventId.tsx` exported its page component (`export function EventDetailPage`), which kept the whole ~16kB route in the initial entry chunk — sibling `donor/$contactId` keeps the component internal and gets its own `_contactId-*.js` chunk. DEFECT-2's added fields pushed the entry past the 862,000 build-output guard, exposing this. Made `EventDetailPage` internal; tests now read it off `Route.component` (the established `$contactId.test.tsx` pattern). Result: a new `_eventId-*.js` chunk appears and the entry dropped **862.9kB → 847.6kB**. Added `eventId` to the build-output route-split guard regex to lock the win. This is the template for the remaining ~50 routes (CODE-SPLIT-01 still OPEN as a dedicated wave).

TDD: new tests for prefill, full-payload save, null-on-clear save, and the delete removeQueries behavior. **Independently verified `@grantpipe/web`: typecheck 0 errors; full suite 184 files / 4314 tests pass; per-file coverage on touched files — `use-events.ts` 100/100/100/100, `$eventId.tsx` 99.74/95.54/100/99.74 (both ≥95).** Pre-commit hook ran its own real gate (exit 0).

### Wave 220 (2026-06-14) — DONE — subrecipient detail edit form (HIGH field-parity gap, DEFECT-2 class)

Closed the highest-priority field-parity gap from the Wave 219 audit: `subrecipients/$subrecipientId.tsx` had **no entity edit form at all** — `name`/`uei`/`status`/`notes` were permanently locked after creation even though the create dialog sets them and `updateSubrecipient` + `updateSubrecipientSchema` already existed and worked.

Status always sent.

### Wave 222 (2026-06-14) — DONE — sidebar scroll-fade affordance (W219 finding #3, discoverability polish)

Closed the minor sidebar fade-affordance candidate logged in Wave 219: `SidebarNav` (`packages/ui/src/components/sidebar.tsx`) is `flex-1 overflow-y-auto` with up to 21 nav items, but on short viewports (all sections expanded) there was **no visual cue that more items lay above/below the scroll fold** — it just looked like the list ended. Directly serves the goal's "an 80-year-old can tell there's more" discoverability bar.

- **JS-detected edge fades.** A `useEffect` reads `scrollTop`/`clientHeight`/`scrollHeight` on scroll + via `ResizeObserver`, deriving `{ up, down }` edge state. A CSS `mask-image` (+ `WebkitMaskImage`) softens whichever edge has off-screen content — top only, bottom only, or both. **No mask when nothing overflows**, so items are never clipped when the list fits. `data-can-scroll-up`/`data-can-scroll-down` attributes expose state (only emitted when `true`). Mask is purely visual — no content removed from the a11y tree, no focus/tab-order impact.
- **Bottom check uses `Math.ceil(scrollTop + clientHeight) < scrollHeight`** to absorb sub-pixel rounding at fractional DPRs (avoids a phantom permanent "down" fade at the very bottom — review-confirmed correct).
- **Pure `scrollMask(up, down)` helper** exported and unit-tested across all four states (neither → `undefined`, top, bottom, both). Dropped the `typeof ResizeObserver !== "undefined"` SSR guard: `packages/ui`'s sidebar is consumed **only by `apps/web`** (client-rendered Vite SPA) — review verified `apps/site` (Astro/SSR) imports no sidebar; `ResizeObserver` is always present in the SPA + stubbed in the ui test-setup. Removing it also kept branch coverage above the per-file gate.

TDD: 8 new tests (4 `scrollMask` states + 4 DOM behavior tests that stub the scroll metrics jsdom doesn't compute, via `Object.defineProperty`). **`@grantpipe/ui` `sidebar.tsx` per-file coverage: 98.65 stmts / 97.72 branch / 100 funcs / 98.65 lines (≥95).** Full ui suite 164 files / 3163 tests pass; web sidebar consumers (`app-sidebar`, `mobile-nav`) 57 tests pass; typecheck 0 errors.

**Review (sub-agent, sonnet): clean — no bugs, no stale-closure/cleanup issues, correct off-by-one handling, SSR-safe given current consumption, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable, no button-canon impact.** One non-blocking note (`scrollMask` is now on the ui public export surface purely for testing) accepted as a reasonable trade-off.

Pre-commit gate ran the real `@grantpipe/ui` typecheck + coverage (cache miss, exit 0). Wave 222 commit `15627e3e` ff-merged to master (worktree reset to local master 127de337 per the base-ref lesson; install + web build before gate). 2 files (+142/-2). - **Design canon:** `file:rounded-full` makes the native button a pill (was a square native control — a canon violation).
- **No regression risk to other inputs:** `file:` utilities only target `::file-selector-button`, which exists solely on `<input type="file">`; text/email/etc. are untouched.

TDD: 1 new test asserting the pill/`appearance-none` utility classes on the rendered file input. `input.tsx` per-file coverage **100/100/100/100**. Full pre-commit gate (affected = ui + web) ran real coverage, exit 0.

One NIT (test queries `document.querySelector("input")`) matches the existing convention in `input.test.tsx` — left as-is.

### Wave 225 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — sidebar `aria-current` exact-match a11y fix

**Defect (a11y, real):** TanStack Router's `<Link>` defaults to **prefix matching**, so a parent nav link (e.g. `/accounting`, label "Overview") was automatically getting `aria-current="page"` + `data-status="active"` while the user was on a child route (e.g. `/accounting/ledger`). Confirmed via live DOM inspection: the Overview anchor reported `aria-current:"page"` on `/app/accounting/ledger`, so **two** nav links announced themselves as the current page to screen readers. The visual `data-active` styling was already correct (driven by the component's own single-source `activePath` computation) — only the anchor's TanStack-supplied `aria-current` leaked.

**Fix** (`apps/web/src/components/shell/app-sidebar.tsx`, in `renderNavItem`'s `<Link>`):

- `activeOptions={{ exact: true }}` — stop TanStack from prefix-marking parent routes.
- `aria-current={isActive ? "page" : undefined}` — mirror the single computed active item (`isActive = item.to === activePath`) so exactly one link announces the current page. Covers mobile nav too (it renders the same `AppSidebar`).

**Tests** (`app-sidebar.test.tsx`): added the primary exact-match leak test + 3 coverage tests (root-path normalization, no-match path, nav-item `onNavigate`); strengthened the active-route test to assert **exactly one** active item (single-active invariant); updated the `Link` mock to expose `activeOptions` as `data-active-exact`. **Documented the scope limit:** the mocked `<Link>` can't replicate TanStack's real prefix matching, so the unit tests verify component-side wiring; runtime suppression is verified via live DOM inspection.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (sonnet) — no blockers; the should-fix (mock can't catch the original bug) + a nit (weak `length>0` assertion) were both addressed in commit `89d826c8`.

**Completion:** both commits gated green (4331 web tests pass; `app-sidebar.tsx` 100% stmts/funcs/lines, 98.27% branches). Commits `f18426ee` (fix) + `89d826c8` (review fixes) ff-merged to master. Worktree removed. `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler.

Also re-walked (read-only, this session) the surfaces the Wave 224 NEXT list still flagged — **all clean, 0 console errors:** Notifications (empty inbox + prefs, "Mark all read" disabled at 0), Integrations (QuickBooks plan-gate), Recurring (empty + New Template), Statement of Activities / Functional Expenses / Financial Position (date-range → Generate), Bank Accounts (Connect empty state), Fiscal Periods (Opening Balances row), Activity log (filters + audit entries + pagination), Calendar (month grid + deadline summary + day panel), Funder detail (inline form + 5 tabs), Fund detail (KPI tiles Allocated $35K/Spent $7K/Balance $28K — math consistent; source allocations + expense ledger), Programs / Subrecipients (plan gates), Reports hub. No new defects.

### Wave 227 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — grant-delete spend-down 404 cache-teardown fix

**Defect (real, found during Wave-226-style grant CRUD E2E):** Deleting a grant fired `GET /api/grants/{id}/spend-down 404 (Not Found)` in the console. Root cause (confirmed via Explore sub-agent): `deleteGrant.onSuccess` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-grants.ts`) removed only the **singular** `["grant", grantId]` cache key, but the grant detail page's Spend-Down and Budget Variance tabs key off the **plural** `["grants", grantId, ...]` prefix — `useSpendDown` → `["grants", id, "spend-down"]` (line ~964); `useGrantBudgetVariance` → `["grants", id, "budget", "variance"]` (line ~978). The broad `["grants"]` invalidate that follows prefix-matched those still-mounted observers and refetched the just-deleted grant → 404.

**Fix:** added `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["grants", grantId] })` immediately after the existing singular `removeQueries` and **before** the broad `invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["grants"] })`, so the deleted detail caches are evicted (no refetch) before the invalidate can re-catch them. Safe vs. the grant LIST cache: the list query key's 2nd element is `params.search` (a string), never a UUID, so the UUID-prefixed `removeQueries` can't prefix-match list caches.

**Test** (`use-grants.test.ts`): extended the existing "removes deleted grant detail from cache instead of invalidating it" test to assert the new `mockRemoveQueries` call with `{ queryKey: ["grants", "grant-1"] }` (TDD: failed first with only the singular call, then passed). 44/44 use-grants tests pass.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (sonnet) — **clean, no issues.** Confirmed: removeQueries (no refetch) is the correct primitive; ordering correct; list cache safe; both `["grants", id, ...]` keys covered by the prefix; test asserts the new call.

**Completion:** gate green on re-run (4331 web tests pass; build-output test required a prior `pnpm --filter @grantpipe/web build` — first run failed only on that precondition, not the fix). Commit `e68a999e` ff-merged to master. Worktree pruned + branch deleted (leftover dir is session-cwd-locked on Windows, harmless — no longer a registered worktree). `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `96b32b9c-0616-4688-bd1b-6c35a0b06567` live on app.grantpipe.com.

NEXT (after Wave 228): see the Wave 232 NEXT block below.

### Wave 228 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — double-submit guard sweep across destructive confirm buttons

**Defect class (real):** A destructive confirm `<Button variant="destructive">` whose `onClick` fires a TanStack `mutateAsync()`/`mutate()` with no `disabled` guard double-fires the mutation on a rapid double-click (two DELETE requests). The canonical guarded reference is the grant delete button (`grants/$grantId.tsx`, `disabled={grantMutations.deleteGrant.isPending}`). A systematic audit of every destructive confirm button in `apps/web` found exactly **3** real network-mutation instances still unguarded:

1. **Funder-level delete** (`funders/$funderId.tsx`) — `disabled={funderMutations.deleteFunder.isPending}` added; onClick fires `deleteFunder.mutateAsync()`.
2. **Funder per-contact delete** (`funders/$funderId.tsx`) — `disabled={contactMutations.deleteContact.isPending}` added; onClick fires `deleteContact.mutateAsync(contact.id)`.
3. **Donor contact delete** (`donors/$contactId.tsx`) — `disabled={deleteContact.isPending}` added; onClick → `handleDelete` fires `deleteContact`.

**Tests (TDD):** added one test per button, each overriding the mock mutation with `isPending: true`, opening the confirm dialog, and asserting the confirm button `toBeDisabled()` (funder: 2 tests, +65 file total; donor: 1 test, +135 file total). Each failed RED before the `disabled` binding, GREEN after.

**Audit result (no change needed — already guarded):** bank account, recurring template, event, bundle item, fund, custom field, document, segment-via-ConfirmDialog (ConfirmDialog already disables its confirm on `isPending`), grant + grant sub-entities, payments, settings/team/portal. Two segment deletes (funds/grants index) are synchronous localStorage — not network mutations, intentionally left alone. `/app/award-intake` 404 confirmed **by-design** (no index route, nothing links to it). Grant-delete spend-down 404 (Wave 227 fix) **re-verified PASS** during the audit.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (lite/sonnet) — **clean, no issues.** Confirmed each `disabled` references the mutation its onClick fires, tests assert the right thing with correct mock overrides, no single-click regression (`isPending` is false until the mutation fires), and no other unguarded destructive confirm button remains in the two files.

**Completion:** 3 commits (`e93d3d88`, `77f85c12`, `cdc97e82`) each gated green, ff-merged to master, re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/web` (6/6 tasks, exit 0 — turbo not on bash PATH directly, use `pnpm exec`). Branch deleted + worktree pruned (leftover dir session-cwd-locked on Windows, harmless). `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `29ecc473-0128-40a1-a1e2-b728d51515f7` live on app.grantpipe.com.

### Wave 229 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — delete confirm dialog keeps open with inline error on failure (funder + fund)

**Defect class (real, HIGH):** A destructive confirm dialog whose `mutateAsync()` rejects must NOT close the dialog in the catch block — doing so discards the user's confirm context and the error is never surfaced where they're looking. Discovered via a web sub-agent E2E error-state walk: the **funder** and **fund** delete dialogs both called `setDeleteOpen(false)` in the catch and routed the message to `saveError` (a banner outside the now-closed dialog). The canonical correct flow is the donor delete (`donors/$contactId.tsx`): a dedicated `deleteError` state set in catch, rendered via `<InlineError>` inside the still-open `<DialogContent>`, cleared in the Dialog's `onOpenChange` on close, dialog never closed on error so the confirm button recovers for retry.

**Source audit result:** ONLY funders and funds had this defect. Grants (`grants/$grantId.tsx` → `setDeleteGrantError`, no close), donors (canonical), and events were already correct — left unchanged.

**Fix (both files):** added `deleteError` state, imported/rendered `InlineError` inside the dialog, `onOpenChange` clears `deleteError` on close, removed `setDeleteOpen(false)` from the catch (now sets `deleteError` only). `saveError` left intact for the save form (not conflated).

- `funders/$funderId.tsx` (+ `$funderId.test.tsx` — new test asserts dialog stays in document, scoped `within(dialog).getByRole("alert")` shows the error, Delete button `toBeEnabled()`; file 66/66).
- `funds/$fundId.tsx` (+ `$fundId.test.tsx` — rewrote the old test that asserted the dialog _closes_ on error into one asserting it stays open with inline error + re-enabled button; added `within` import; file 35/35).

**TDD:** both regression tests confirmed RED against unfixed code, GREEN after the fix.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (sonnet) — **clean, no issues.** Confirmed catch no longer closes dialog, `deleteError` cleared on close (no stale-error-on-reopen), `InlineError` inside `DialogContent`, no `saveError`/`deleteError` conflation, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable, tests assert open+error+re-enabled, consistent with donor canon.

**Completion:** commit `44fb1f5e` gated green (5/5 + 1/1, exit 0), ff-merged to master, re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/web` (6/6 tasks, 4335 web tests pass, exit 0). Branch deleted + worktree pruned (leftover dir session-cwd-locked on Windows, harmless). `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `ffd3426b-723c-417e-9af4-a1cb13974081` live on app.grantpipe.com.

### Wave 230 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — one-click Retry on list error banners + donor pagination-on-error fix

**Defect class (real, MEDIUM — UX/recoverability):** Five top-level domain LIST pages (donors, grants, funds, funders, events) rendered a destructive `<Alert>` on a failed fetch with copy telling the user to "refresh the page" — no in-place recovery. A reusable `apps/web/src/components/retry-button.tsx` (`<RetryButton query={q} />` → calls `q.refetch()`, disabled while `q.isFetching`, shows "Retrying...") already existed and was used by payments/index, subrecipients/index, reports/index, but NOT by these five. Separately, the **donors** list-view pagination ("Page 1 of 1") rendered even when the query errored.

**Source audit (Explore sub-agent):** confirmed all five lists had the error alert but no retry; only donors had the pagination-on-error bug (funds/funders/events already guarded with `!isErrorState`/`!query.isError`; grants' pagination was inside the non-error else branch so safe).

**Fix (one commit `10ad9069`):** wired `<RetryButton query={<thatList'sQuery>} />` inside each list's error branch (donors has two — mobile + desktop — both fixed); guarded donors list-view pagination with `&& !isErrorState` (`isErrorState = contactsQuery.isError === true`). RetryButton passes the SAME query whose error triggered the branch.

### Wave 231 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — donor delete made atomic (journal reversal moved inside the transaction)

**Defect class (real, HIGH — data integrity):** The 409 fiscal-period error seen during donor-detail delete (Wave 229 finding #3) was NOT a benign background-query leak — it was a genuine **partial-commit** bug. `deleteContact` (`apps/api/src/domains/donors/contact.service.ts`) ran `db.transaction(...)` to soft-delete the contact + its donations + junction rows + activity log, then **after** the transaction committed it looped over the affected donations calling `postDonation(db, { action: "delete" })` to reverse their journal entries. When `postDonation` threw a 409 (`conflict("No open fiscal period covers ...")` from `postingEngine.ts → findOpenFiscalPeriod`, raised when reversing entries whose original fiscal period is closed and no open period covers the reversal date), the contact + donations were already soft-deleted but the journal entries were left **unreversed** — dangling ledger state, plus a confusing 409 surfacing after the record visibly disappeared.

**Root cause:** the standalone single-donation delete (`donation.service.ts:~211`) already calls `postDonation(tx, ...)` INSIDE its transaction (atomic). The donor delete called it with `db` AFTER the transaction (non-atomic). Inconsistent pattern.

**Fix (one commit `01d94c42`):** moved the affected-donation-ID collection AND the `postDonation` reversal loop INSIDE the transaction, passing `tx` (not `db`), matching the donation-delete pattern. The ID select now runs inside the tx BEFORE the donations are soft-deleted (so `isNull(deletedAt)` still captures them). A reversal failure now rolls the entire delete back — contact + donations remain, no dangling journal state — and the 409 is surfaced atomically and recoverably (and the Wave 229 donor delete dialog now keeps open + shows that 409 inline, so the user gets a clear, actionable message: open a fiscal period covering the date, then retry).

**TDD:** `contact.service.test.ts` — moved the donation-ID select mock onto the `tx` mock; updated existing assertions to expect `postDonation` called with the tx context (not `db`); ADDED an atomicity test proving `deleteContact` REJECTS when `postDonation` throws inside the transaction (modeled on the tag.service rollback test). 65 tests pass; `donation.service.test.ts` (31) untouched & green. Coverage on contact.service.ts: 100% stmts / 97.72% branches / 100% fns / 100% lines.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (sonnet) — **clean, no defects.** Confirmed select-before-soft-delete ordering, loop inside tx with `tx`+actorId guard+action:delete, old post-tx loop & pre-tx select fully removed, `notFound` still thrown after tx, all in-tx operations preserved in sensible order, atomicity test load-bearing, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable.

**Completion:** commit `01d94c42` gated green (3/3 + 1/1, exit 0), ff-merged to master, re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/api` (4/4 tasks, exit 0). Branch deleted + worktree pruned (leftover dir session-cwd-locked on Windows, harmless). `grantpipe-api-production` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `eee5fb28-9f77-4b10-95aa-f6650cf3beed` live on app.grantpipe.com/api/\*.

### Wave 232 (2026-06-15) — DONE (audit-only, CLEAN) — codebase-wide audit for the post-transaction side-effect anti-pattern

**Motivation:** Wave 231 fixed a partial-commit bug where `deleteContact` reversed journal entries via `postDonation(db, ...)` AFTER its `db.transaction(...)` committed. This wave audited all of `apps/api/src/domains` for the same anti-pattern — any DB-mutating or throw-capable call (especially posting-engine functions) performed after a transaction resolves, operating on `db` instead of `tx`.

**Method (Explore sub-agent):** enumerated every `db.transaction(` call site; grepped every posting-engine call site (`postDonation`, `postExpense`, `postGrantPayment`, `reverseGrantPayment`, `postGrantCloseout`); checked each function for post-transaction writes.

**Result: CLEAN — 0 HIGH, 0 MEDIUM findings.** Every posting-engine call site passes `tx`/`txDb` inside its transaction (donation create/update/delete, contact delete [the Wave 231 fix], event attendee, import row processing, accounting seed, fund/planned-expense, grant closeout, payment post/reverse). The only post-transaction work found elsewhere is fire-and-forget analytics (`captureBillingAnalytics`/`captureExternalReviewEvent` — no DB writes, errors swallowed) and `document-extractions` writes that live in a `catch` block on a ROLLED-BACK transaction (error recovery, not post-commit). All NON-ISSUE. **Conclusion: the anti-pattern was isolated to `deleteContact` and does not recur.** No code change; this is a confirmation/coverage result.

NEXT (resume here): atomicity anti-pattern confirmed isolated & fixed. Continue real workflows on deeper **detail** surfaces (award-intake detail via direct nav, evidence-bundle detail, payment/request detail) and more **error/loading states** across the app. Backlog still holds **CODE-SPLIT-01** (~50 routes; verify real bundle savings first). Minor enhancement (not a defect): list **cards are sparse** vs. the dense grants table.

### Wave 233 (2026-06-15) — DONE (HIGH defect fixed & deployed) — grant detail Overview funder + pipeline-status Selects rendered empty despite saved data

**Symptom (confirmed live via Playwright on the real local stack, grant `1bbea88e` PASSPORT Home Care Services):** the Overview tab's **Funder** Select showed the "Select funder" placeholder and the **Pipeline status** Select showed nothing — even though the grant has funder "Ohio Department of Aging" (`dab647e9`) and status "reporting" (API response + the sidebar Linked-context panel both render the funder correctly). Both Radix hidden native selects sat on their _first_ option (the unmatched default), proving the controlled `value` reaching each Select was empty.

**Root cause (found by reading the live React fiber hook state):** both drafts were `{dirty:true, value:""}`. Each Select (`apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated/grants/$grantId.tsx`) uses a "preserve user edits, sync loaded data" pattern — a `{dirty, grantId, value}` draft with a `useEffect` that syncs the loaded grant value UNLESS `dirty` is true. Radix Select emits a **spurious empty-value `onValueChange`** while its items/value settle on load; that fired `setDraft({dirty:true, value:""})`, and the `if (current.dirty || ...) return current` guard then **permanently blocked** the load-sync effect. (Not reproducible in the existing jsdom tests because they mock Radix away — the mock never emits the stray empty change.)

**Fix (one commit `d97ebb1c`):** guard both `onValueChange` handlers with `if (!value) return;`. An empty funder/status is never a valid user selection (both item lists — `funders.map` and `GRANT_STAGE_DETAILS.map` — are non-empty with no clear/empty option), so ignoring an empty change is safe and breaks the dirty-lock. Verified live with the same edit HMR'd into the dev server: funder now shows "Ohio Department of Aging", status "Reporting"; main repo reverted clean before merging the worktree.

**TDD:** added a faithful regression test to `$grantId.test.tsx` — renders with the grant loaded (Selects show saved values), fires a stray empty `onValueChange` on both comboboxes, and asserts the saved values SURVIVE (red without the guard, green with it). Plus two cold-load/StrictMode sync sanity tests. Full file suite 168/168 green.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (haiku/lite) — **PASS.** Confirmed fix correctness, no legitimate empty-selection path, faithful regression test, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable/non-pill buttons.

**Completion:** commit `d97ebb1c` gated green (web typecheck + coverage, exit 0), ff-merged to master, re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/web` (6/6 tasks, exit 0). Branch deleted + worktree pruned (leftover dir harmless on Windows). `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `c941b5ed-d551-47f8-90e4-47a31c08215f` live on app.grantpipe.com.

### Wave 234 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — grant Spend-Down "By Month" rendered raw ISO month keys

**Symptom (triaged from the Wave 233 E2E-walk list):** the grant detail Spend-Down tab's "By Month" table rendered each row's month as the raw ISO key `2025-12` / `2026-01` instead of a human label like `Dec 2025`. `row.month` comes from `spend-down.service.ts` as `expense.date.toISOString().substring(0, 7)` ("YYYY-MM"); the table at `$grantId.tsx:3068` dumped it verbatim. Cosmetic but visibly unpolished on a compliance surface auditors read.

**Fix (commit `be493749`):** added an exported `formatYearMonthLabel(value)` helper in `$grantId.tsx` mirroring the existing `formatIsoDateLabel` style — splits "YYYY-MM", validates finite year/month, month 1–12, and 4-digit year, then formats via `Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {timeZone:"UTC", year:"numeric", month:"short"})` → "Dec 2025". Falls back to the raw value for malformed input and returns "" for nullish. Used at the "By Month" `<td>`.

**TDD:** RED first — added unit tests for the helper (valid keys, malformed/out-of-range fallback, nullish→"") and updated the byMonth render test to assert "Jan 2026"/"Feb 2026" and NOT the raw ISO keys. Confirmed red (`formatYearMonthLabel is not a function`), then green. Full `$grantId.test.tsx` suite 171/171.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (haiku/lite) — correctness PASS (off-by-one/timezone clean, matches `formatIsoDateLabel` conventions, no quality-gate violations); flagged thin validation-branch coverage, addressed with extra tests in commit `3e164e31` (month 0/13, short year, null/undefined).

**Completion:** both commits gated green (web typecheck + coverage, exit 0), ff-merged to master (`3e164e31`), re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/web` (6/6 tasks, exit 0). ### Wave 235 (2026-06-15) — DONE & DEPLOYED — grant Reporting "Mark submitted" hidden on already-submitted rows

**Triage of the Wave 233 observation list (sonnet Explore agent, read-only against source):** of the five remaining grant-detail observations, only ONE was a real defect:

- **Tab-list overflow — NOT a defect.** `packages/ui/.../tabs.tsx:36` `variant="record"` already applies `overflow-x-auto flex-nowrap [scrollbar-width:thin]`; `$grantId.tsx` uses `variant="record"` and every trigger has `shrink-0`. Scrollable. (No scroll-fade like the sidebar, but functional — cosmetic only.)
- **Lifecycle stepper highlighting — NOT a defect.** `GrantStageProgressStrip` (`$grantId.tsx:147-209`) correctly computes `currentIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(currentStatus)` and `isCurrent = !isTerminal && currentIndex === idx`, highlighting the active stage from `grant.status`. Terminal statuses (declined/renewal) handled as a trailing badge.
- **Reporting "Mark submitted" on submitted rows — REAL DEFECT (fixed this wave).**
- **Evidence-bundles breadcrumb — NOT a defect.** `evidence-bundles/$bundleId.tsx:271-283` renders "Evidence bundles" → `{bundle.title}`; both correct.

**Defect symptom:** the Reporting tab rendered the "Mark submitted" `<Button>` for every requirement row unconditionally (`$grantId.tsx:2709`), even when `requirement.derivedStatus === "submitted"`. Clicking it on an already-submitted row fired a no-op `updateRequirement({status:"submitted"})` — a dead affordance that clutters the row and undermines the "states are unambiguous, actions have clear outcomes" design principle on a compliance surface.

**Fix (commit `830056dd`):** wrap the button in `{requirement.derivedStatus !== "submitted" ? (<Button…/>) : null}`. `derivedStatus` is `upcoming|in_progress|submitted|overdue` (optional); `!== "submitted"` correctly shows the button for every non-submitted state including `undefined`. The status badge and "Delete requirement" button still render for submitted rows (row stays intact, only the redundant action is removed). Hiding (not disabling) matches the file's pervasive ternary-render pattern.

**TDD:** RED first — added "hides the Mark submitted button for a requirement that is already submitted" (asserts the button is absent but Delete requirement remains) + "shows the Mark submitted button for a requirement that is not yet submitted". Confirmed red (button found when it should be hidden), then green. Full `$grantId.test.tsx` suite 173/173.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (haiku/lite) — **CLEAN.** Confirmed `!== "submitted"` correctness across all states incl. undefined, hiding matches surrounding patterns, row not blanked, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable/non-pill buttons, both cases covered.

**Completion:** commit `830056dd` gated green (web typecheck + coverage, exit 0), ff-merged to master, re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/web` (6/6 tasks, exit 0). - subrecipient detail: replaced the bare loading div with a `Skeleton`-based loading state (`data-testid="subrecipient-detail-loading"`, kicker/title/description + two card skeletons), matching the sibling pattern. Added `Skeleton` to the `@grantpipe/ui` import.

## Wave 237 — Humanize remaining raw enums + surface swallowed action errors (deployed)

**Audit (sonnet Explore agent) of workbench detail surfaces for raw-enum / swallowed-error / bare-loading patterns. Findings triaged against source:**

- **Funder detail grant-history status badge rendered raw enum — REAL.** `funders/$funderId.tsx` grant-history rows showed `active` (with a `capitalize` class crutch) instead of a properly humanized label.
- **Accounting integrations latest-run status rendered raw enum — REAL.** `accounting/integrations.tsx` Review Queue "Latest run" showed `completed - 12 imported` instead of `Completed - 12 imported`.
- **Bank account transaction status badge rendered raw enum — REAL.** `accounting/bank/$bankAccountId.tsx` transaction rows showed `unmatched`/`matched`/`ignored` (with `capitalize`) instead of humanized labels.
- **Grant spend-down report generation error swallowed — REAL.** `grants/$grantId.tsx` `.catch(() => undefined)` silently dropped failures generating a compliance spend-down report.
- **Grant AI award-intake extraction error swallowed — REAL.** `grants/$grantId.tsx` `AwardIntakeDocumentAction` `.catch(() => undefined)` silently dropped AI-intake start failures.

**Fix (commit `152b77ef`):**

- funders detail: `<Badge variant="outline">{humanizeEnum(grant.status)}</Badge>` (removed the `capitalize` crutch); imported `humanizeEnum`.
- accounting integrations: `` `${humanizeEnum(latestRun.status)} - ${latestRun.importedCount} imported` ``; imported `humanizeEnum`.
- bank account detail: `<Badge variant={variants[status] ?? "outline"}>{humanizeEnum(status)}</Badge>` (removed `capitalize`); imported `humanizeEnum`.
- grant detail: new `spendDownReportError` state reset before each attempt, set on `.catch`, rendered in a `StatusPanel variant="error"`.
- grant detail: `AwardIntakeDocumentAction` gained local `intakeError` state (reset → set → rendered as `text-xs text-destructive`), replacing the swallow.

**TDD:** RED first — funders asserts "Active" present / "active" absent; integrations asserts "Completed - 12 imported"; bank asserts humanized status badges (getAllByText since filter Select reuses humanized labels) + lowercase absent; grant asserts spend-down error surfaced + AI-intake error surfaced (and navigate not called). Confirmed red, then green. Note: adding a `humanizeEnum` usage to a source file that has a test mocking `lib/format` requires adding the export to that mock — funders test factory updated accordingly.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (haiku/lite) — **No issues found.** Confirmed `humanizeEnum` imported in each touched source, error states reset to null before each attempt, error renders reachable, no `any`/TODO/eslint-disable/non-pill buttons.

## Wave 241 — Live E2E visual audit (stack up) + naming/pill re-walk

Brought up the local stack (docker pg on 55439 already running, `.dev.vars` present, `pnpm dev:server start all` on web 3050 / api 5050; health + get-session both 200 — no stale-wrangler 500). Logged in as demo and ran a Playwright `web` sub-agent over all major screens (dashboard, donors, grants+tabs, funds, funders, events, programs, reports, payments, accounting, activity) at desktop + 390px mobile.

**Triage (REAL = fix; REJECTED = not a defect):**

- **AUDIT-1 (HIGH, REAL):** Grant detail `record` TabsList holds 14 tabs; at 1280px scrollWidth 1458 > clientWidth 975, so Activity + Documents tabs are off-screen with no scroll affordance (thin scrollbar only). Users miss whole tabs. → Wave 242.
- **AUDIT-2 (HIGH, REAL):** Dashboard "Restricted Balance Risk" — four "Release is missing evidence" rows are textually identical (no fund/restriction name/date), can't tell which is which; fifth row uses a non-actionable "Missing Evidence" badge while others have "Release Without Support" buttons → inconsistent affordance. Needs per-row context. → backlog.
- **AUDIT-3 (HIGH, REJECTED):** "Get help" FAB overlaps accounting table rows. Per memory [[project_help-fab-overlap-defect]] this corner-overlap is standard vendored-widget behavior — wontfix. Not re-opened.
- **AUDIT-4 (MED, low-confidence):** Mobile List/Board toggle reportedly clipped by FAB — FAB is bottom-right, toggle top-right; unlikely to overlap except on very short pages. Re-verify before acting. → backlog.
- **AUDIT-5 (MED, REJECTED):** `/app/cash` 404 — invented URL; nav label "Cash" correctly routes to `/payments`, which is consistent across nav + page title + breadcrumb + command palette (confirmed in source). No user types `/cash`. Not a defect.
- **AUDIT-6 (MED, REAL):** Funders card grid truncates long funder names (e.g. "U.S. Dept. of Health & Human Ser…") with no `title`/tooltip → no way to read full name. Easy fix. → backlog.
- **AUDIT-7 (LOW, REAL):** ~60 TanStack Router "named export prevents code-split" console warnings on every load = existing CODE-SPLIT-01 backlog item. Deferred (verify real bundle savings first).
- **AUDIT-8 (LOW, REAL):** Accounting Overview "Open Fiscal Period: None" stat card is a dead end — no link to create a period. Easy fix. → backlog.
- **AUDIT-9 (LOW, needs investigation):** Accounting "Net Assets" shows $0 across all classes while Dashboard fund balances are non-zero. Likely correct (seed has no posted journal entries; net assets derive from GL, fund balances from allocations — different subsystems) but reads as broken; no UI explanation. Investigate in a dedicated accounting wave before changing anything.

**Pill compliance re-walk:** CLEAN. Grepped source for raw `<button>`/clickable elements with non-pill rounding → zero. Sub-agent confirmed computed `border-radius` on all buttons = rounded-full. Canon holds.

NEXT (resume here): Wave 242 = fix AUDIT-1 (grant tab overflow scroll affordance) via the shared `record` TabsList variant so all overflowing tablists benefit. Then work AUDIT-6, AUDIT-8 (quick), AUDIT-2 (medium), AUDIT-4 (re-verify first), AUDIT-9 (investigate). Local stack is UP (web 3050 / api 5050, demo@grantpipe.com) — use it for live verification.

## Wave 242 — Scroll-aware edge fades on record TabsList (deployed) — fixes AUDIT-1

**Fixed AUDIT-1 (grant detail's 14-tab `record` TabsList overflows with no visible scroll affordance) at the shared component, so all 9 `variant="record"` call sites benefit with zero call-site churn.** `packages/ui/src/components/tabs.tsx` gains a `useTabsOverflow` hook and a `RecordTabsList` wrapper; `TabsList` now delegates to it when `variant === "record"`. The wrapper renders two `aria-hidden` gradient fade spans (left/right) that fade in only when content is scrolled past that edge, signaling "more tabs this way." The hook tracks `scrollLeft`/`scrollWidth`/`clientWidth` via a `scroll` listener + `ResizeObserver`, toggling `data-visible` on each fade.

**Coverage gotcha (callback-ref pattern):** the first cut used `useRef` + `if (!el) return` guards, which left the null branch uncovered (ref always present) → branch coverage 83.33% < 95% gate. Refactored to a **callback-ref + `useState`** hook: the effect runs once with `node===null` (initial render, covers the early-return TRUE branch) then again with the element (FALSE branch) — both branches covered with no extra scaffolding. tabs.tsx hit **100%** stmts/branch/funcs/lines.

**Test gotcha (ResizeObserver is a no-op mock):** `packages/ui/src/test-setup.ts` stubs ResizeObserver as a no-op, so fade-toggle tests are driven by `scroll` events with manually-set scroll metrics (`Object.defineProperty` helper `setScrollMetrics`) dispatched inside `act`. Two new tests (8 total in tabs.test.tsx): renders the scroll wrapper + both fade spans for the record variant; toggles fade visibility across 4 scroll positions (start→end-fade-only, middle→both, end→start-fade-only, no-overflow→neither). All pass.

**Review:** sub-agent code review (lite/haiku) — **No issues found.**

**Completion:** commit `bc644bc8` gated green (full @grantpipe/ui typecheck + coverage, COMMIT_EXIT=0). ff-merged to master (`3146f2c1..bc644bc8`). Re-verified post-merge with `pnpm exec turbo typecheck test --force --filter=@grantpipe/ui` (3/3 tasks, VERIFY_EXIT=0). Live E2E verification PASS (fades toggle correctly on the grant detail tabs, Activity/Documents reachable, 0 console errors). Worktree `tabs-scroll-fade` removed + branch deleted. `grantpipe-web` deployed via Wrangler — Version ID `c3dd03db-93a8-4516-9ab3-997cc9462724` live on app.grantpipe.com.
